2a1(rgyud): @##//maha bA+ai ro tsa na/a b+hi sam b+ho d+hi/bi kur bi ta a d+hi sh+sha na/pi pul l+ya su tre/na tra ra tsa na ma d+ha rmangs rgya yang;
2a2(rgyud): bod skad du;rnam par snang mdzad chen po mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa/rnam par sprul pa byin gyis rlob pa;shin tu
2a3(rgyud): rgyas pa mdo sde'i dbang po'i rgyal po zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam gangs/bcom ldan 'das ngag gi dbang phyug la phyag 
2a4(rgyud): 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis
2a5(rgyud): byin gyis rlob pa/rdo rje chen po rgya che ba chos kyi dbyings kyi pho brang/rdo rje 'dzin thams cad 'du ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i
2a6(rgyud): mos pa rnam par rol pa/mtha' dang dbus med cing rtse mo mtho ba rin po che'i rgyal pos shin du legs par brgyan pa;;
2b1(rgyud): 'byung ba'i khang bzangs chen po rin po che'i rgyal po'i nang na/byang chub sems dpa' lus kyi seng ge'i khri la bzhugs te;'di
2b2(rgyud): lta ste rdo rje 'dzin /namakha' dri ma med pa dang/namakha' la rnam par 'gro ba dang/namakha' la 'byung ba dang/gos sna tshogs
2b3(rgyud): gyon pa dang/sna tshogs su rgyu ba dang/chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid la gnas pa dang/sems can gyi khams mtha' yas pa
2b4(rgyud): dang;yongs su skyob pa'i rdo rje dang/srid med kyi bu'i stobs can dang /rdo rje bzang dang/rdo rje mchog gi shugs 'chang ba dang;;
2b5(rgyud): dri ma med pa'i rdo rje mchog gi 'chang ba dang/de bzhin gshegs pa'i go cha dang/de bzhin gshegs pa go 'phang du 'byung ba dang/
2b6(rgyud): spros pa med pa la gnas pa dang/de bzhin gshegs pa' stobs bcu las nges par skyes pa dang/dri ma med pa'i mig dang/gsang
2b7(rgyud): ba'i bdag po phyag na rdo rje dang/de dag laswogs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul snyed dang/thabs gcig tu bzhugs te
3a1(rgyud): @##//byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po dang/byams pa dang/'jam dpal dang/sgrib pa thams cad rnam par sel
3a2(rgyud): ba laswogs pa'i de dag gis kyang/skor cing 'dun gyis ltas pa la 'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i nyi ma dus gsum las 'das par
3a3(rgyud): byin gyis brlabs nas/sku dang gsung dang thugs mnyam pa nyid kyi gzhi zhes bya ba'i chos kyi sgo'i chos ston to//de nas byang chub sems dpa'
3a4(rgyud): kun tu bzang po laswogs pa de dag dang/gsang ba'i bdag po laswogs pa rdo rje 'dzin de dag/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi byin gyis
3a5(rgyud): brlabs kyis sku mnyam pa nyid la bkod pa mi zad pa'i mdzod rnam par 'gyings pa la zhugs so//de bzhin du gsung dang thugs mnyam pa'i nyid bkod
3a6(rgyud): pa mi zad pa'i rnam par 'gyings pa la zhus te/bcom ldan 'das rnam pa snang mdzad kyi sku 'am/gsung ngam thugs la ni 'jug pa 'am;
3a7(rgyud): ldog pa yang mi dmigs so//thams cad du yang bcom ldan 'das rnam par sang mdzad kyi sku'i 'phrin las thams cad dang/gsung gi 'phrin
3b1(rgyud): las thams cad dang/thugs kyi 'phran las thams cad dang/sems can gyi khams thams cad la kun du gsang sngags kyi tshul gyi tshig 'di dag
3b2(rgyud): gis chos ston par mthong ba ni/'di lta ste/sems can las kyi tshe rnam par bskyed pa rnams sems dang po bskyed pa nas bzung ste/sa bcu pa'i
3b3(rgyud): bar gnas pa 'di nyid yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang/las kyi tshe kag pa rnams yang srid pa'i myugu mngon par 'grub par bya ba'i phyir;rdo rje 'dzin
3b4(rgyud): pa'i lus 'dra ba dang/byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po dang/phyag na pad ma'i lus 'dra ba phyogs bcu na gsang sngags kyi tshul gi tshig rnam
3b5(rgyud): par dag pa'i tshig dag gis chos ston pa dag kyang mthong ngo//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la gsang ba po'i bdag po phyag na rdo rje 'khor 'dus pa de
3b6(rgyud): nyid na 'dug pas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das ci ltar de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas
3b7(rgyud): rnams kyis thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes snyes nas/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye she de sems can rnams la rnam par phye ste//tshul sna
4a1(rgyud): @#//tshogs dang/bsam pa sna tshogs dang/thabs sna tshogs dag gis ston cing /kha cig la ni nyan thos kyi theg pa'i tshul/kha cig
4a2(rgyud): la ni rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa'i tshul dang/kha cig la ni theg pa chen po'i tshul /kha cig la ni mngon par shes pa lnga'i tshul/kha cig la ni ltar
4a3(rgyud): skyes par bya ba dang/kha cig la ni mir skye bar bya ba dang/lto mche chen po dang/klu dang gnod sbyin dang/dri za dang /lha ma yin dang/namakha'
4a4(rgyud): lding dang/mi 'am cir skye ba'i bar chos ston lags/de la sems can la la sangs rgyas kyis 'dul bar bya dag gis ni sangs rgyas kyi gzugs
4a5(rgyud): su mthong;;la las ni nyan thos kyi gzugs su/la las ni rang sangs rgyas kyi gzugs su/la las  ni byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs su;;
4a6(rgyud): la las ni dbang phyug chen pa'i gzugs su/la las ni tshangs pa'i gzugs su/la las  nisred med kyi bu'i gzugs su/la las nirnam thos
4a7(rgyud): kyi bu'i gzugs su/la la ni lto 'phye chen po dang/mi dang mi ma yin pa'i bar du mthong ste/rang rang gi tshig brjod pa'i tshul dang/spyod lam sna
4b1(rgyud): tshogs mthong la/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes de yang 'di ltar /de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par grol ba'i rong ro gcig la lags/bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste/
4b2(rgyud): dper bgyi na;namakha'i khams ni rtog pa dang/rnam par rtog pa thams cad dang bral ba ste/mi rtog mi rtog pa lags so/ca/bcom ldan 'das 'di lha ste dper bgyi na;sa
4b3(rgyud): chen po 'di ni sems can gyi gsos su gyur pa lags so//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin du thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shas kyang/lha dang mi dang lha ma yan du bcas pa'i 'jig
4b4(rgyud): rten gyi gsos su gyur pa lag so//bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste dper bgyi na/ma'i khams ni bud shing thams cad sregs pas/nam yang yongs su bas par mi 'gyur 
4b5(rgyud): lags so//de bzhin du thams cad mkhye na pa'i ye shes kyang /lha dang mi dang lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyi gsos su 'gyur ro//mi shes pa'i bud shing thams cad
4b6(rgyud): sregs pas/nam yang yong su bas par mi 'gyur lags so//bcom ldan 'angs 'di lta ste dper bgyi ni/rlung gi khams ni rdul rnyed thams cad sel bar bgyid pa lagaso
4b7(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das de bzhinadu thams cad mkhyen pa'i ya shes kyang /mi shes pa'i rdul thams cad sel bar bgyi da pa lags so//bcom lda 'das 'di lta ste dper gyi na;
4b8(rgyud): chu'i khams ni sems can thams cad kyi lus sim par bgyid pa lags so//de bzhin du thabs cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyang/lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten du sim par bgyid
5a1(rgyud): @#//pa lags na;bcom ldan 'das ye shes 'di ni rgyu ni gang lags/rtsa ba ni gang lags/mthar thug pa ni gang lags/de skad ces gsol pa dang/bcom ldan 'das rnam
5a2(rgyud): par snang mdzad kyis/phyag na rdo rje la 'di skad ces bka' stsal pa/lag na rdo rje legs so legaso//lag na rdo rje gang khyod nga la don 'di 'dri ba ni legaso//de'i phyir nyon la legs par yid
5a3(rgyud): la zung shig/da ngas khyod la bshad do//rgyu ni byang chub kyis so//rtsa ba ni snying rje chen po'o//mthar thug pa ni thabs so//gsang ba'i bdag po de la byang chub gang zhe na/rang gi seMs
5a4(rgyud): yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin yongasu shes pa ste/de yang bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub po//gsang ba'i bdag po de la ni chos rdul tsam yang med cing mi dmig+sa
5a5(rgyud): so;;de ci'i phyir zhe na;byang chub de ni namakha'i mtshan nyid do//de yang byang chub par byed pa yang med la/byang chub bar bya ba 'ga' yang med do//de ci'i phyir zhe na;byang chu
5a6(rgyud): la mtshan nyid med pa'i phyir te/gsang ba pa'i bdag po thams cad kyang mtshan nyid med bar 'di lta ste/namakha'i mtshan nyid do//de nas bcom ldan 'das la phyag na rdo rjes 'di skad
5a7(rgyud): ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ni gang nas yongasu btsal bar bgyi//gang gis ni byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar bgyi/bcom ldan
5a8(rgyud): 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/gsang ba'i bdag po ni byang chub dang thams cad mkhyen pa nyid na/gang nas yongasu btsal bar mi bgyi/gang gis byang chub mngon par rdzogasapar
5b1(rgyud): 'tshang rgya bar bgyi/de ci'i phyir zhe na/sems de ni rang bzhin gyis yongs su dag pa yin te/de ni nang na yang med/phyi rol na yang mi gnas/gnyi ga'i bar na yang mi dmigs so//
5b2(rgyud): gsang ba po'i bdag po sems de ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis ma gzigs/gzigs par mi 'gyur te/sngon po ma yin;ser po
5b3(rgyud): ma yin;dmar po ma yin/dkar po ma yin/kha dog leb rgan du ma yin/shel gyi kha dog tu ma yin/thung ngur ma yin/ring por ma yin /zlum por ma yin/gru bzhir ma yin/
5b4(rgyud): snang bar ma yin/mun par ma yin/skyes par ma yin/bud med du ma yin/ma ning du ma yin/gsang ba po'i bdag po sem ni 'dod pa'i khams kyi rang bzhin ma yin;gzugs
5b5(rgyud): kyi khams kyi rang bzhin ma yi /gzugs med pa'i khams kyi rang bzhin ma yis ne//lha dang klu dang gnod sbyin dang/dri za dang lha ma yin dang/mi 'am ci dang lto 'phye
5b6(rgyud): chen po dang;mi dang mi ma yin pa'i 'gro ba sems can ris mthun pa'i rang bzhin ma yin no//gsang ba po'i bdagpo sems de ni mig la mi gnas/rna ba la ma yin;sna la
5b7(rgyud): ma yin;lce la ma yin;lus la ma yin/yid la mi gnas so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/sems de ni nam mkha'i mtshan nyid do//de'i phyir rtog pa dang/rnam par rtog pa thams cad dang bral
5b8(rgyud): ba'o;;de ci'i phyir zhe na/nam mkha'i rang bzhin gang yin pa de ni/sems kyi rang bzhin no//sems kyi rang bzhin gang yin pa da ni byang chub kyi rang bzhin yin te/gsang ba po'i bdag
6a1(rgyud): @#//po de ltar na/sems dang nam mkha'i dbyings dang 'di ni gnyisu med de gnyisu byar med do//de yang snying rje'i rtsa ba can thabs kyis pha rol du phyin pa yongasu bzung ba ste/gsang 
6a2(rgyud): ba'i bdag po de bas na/rnam grangs 'dis kyang 'di ltar/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi nang na chos gang dang gang ston pa de dag thams cad ni byang chub kyi sems yongasu dag par bya ba dang/
6a3(rgyud): sems yongasu shes par bya ba'i phyir yin par rig par bya'o//gsang ba po'i bdag po de ltar bas na/rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo byang chub yongasu shes par 'dod pas rang gi sems nyid 
6a4(rgyud): yongasu shes par bya'o//gsang ba po' bdag po ci ltar rang gi sems yongasu shes par bya ste/'di lta ste rnam par ram/kha dog tu 'am/yul du 'am/gzugasu 'am/tshor 
6a5(rgyud): bar 'am/'du shes su 'am/'du byed du 'am/rnam par shes pa 'am/bdag tu 'am/bdag gir ram/bzung ba 'am/dag par ram/mi dag par ram/khams su 'am 
6a6(rgyud): skye mched du 'am/rnam pa thams cad kyi bar du yongasu btsal kyang mi snyed pa yin te/gsang ba po'i bdag po 'di ni byang chub sems dpa'i byang chub kyi sems rnam par dag pa'i sgo zhes bya ba 
6a7(rgyud): chos snang ba'i sgo'i tshul dang po ste/gang la gnas na byang chub sems dpa' rnams tshegs chung ngus sgrib pa thams spong ba'i ting nge 'dzin thob cing /de thob pas byang chub sems dpa' rnam 
6a8(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad dang/lhan cig 'grogs pa thob par 'gyur ro//mngon par shes pa lnga dag kyang thob par 'gyur ro//skad dang sgra dang /dbyangs mthu 'ams pa'i gzugs kyang thob par 
6b1(rgyud): 'gyur;sems can gyi bsam pa rjes su shes pa dang/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis/'khor ba na yongasu mi nyam pa'i chos can du 'gyur/sems can gyi don bya ba dag
6b2(rgyud): la yongasu mi sgyu par 'gyur/'dus ma byas kyi tshul khrims las rab tu gnas pa dang/log par lta ba rnam par spangs pa dang/yang dag par lta ba khong du chud par 'gyur pa yin no//gsang ba po'i bdag po
6b3(rgyud): gzhan yang sgrib pa thams cad spong ba'i ti nge 'dzin 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni mos pa'i stobs bskyed pas/tshegs chung dus sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yongasu rdzogs par byed do;
6b4(rgyud): gsang ba po'i bdag po 'dor na rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo de ni yon tan dpag tu med pa dang ldan par 'gyur ro//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la phyag na rdo rjes yongasu zhus pa/bcom ldan
6b5(rgyud): 'das ci ltar byang chub tu sems bskyed pa zhes bgyi ba lags/byang chub 'byung ba'i sems bskyed pa'i mtshan nyid ci ltar 'tshal bar bgyi/sems mkhyen pa ni rnams kyi mchog//
6b6(rgyud): dpa' chen ye shes las byung ba//ci snyed cig las 'das nas ni//skye bar 'gyur ba bstan du gsol//mgon po sems kyi mtshan nyid dang//dus kyang rgya cher bshad du gsol//de dag bsod nas
6b7(rgyud): ci 'dra ba dang//spyod pa ci 'drar 'gyur ba dang//sems la sems kyi khyad par yang//thub pa chen po bstan du gsol//de skad gsol nas de'i tshe//bcom ldan rnam snang mdzad chen gyis//phyag na
6b8(rgyud): rdo rje cher 'byung la//legs so bu zhes 'di skad gsungs//theg pa chen po'i go 'phang mchog//sems kyi rgyud kyi mtshan nyid ni//rdzogs sangs rgyas kyi gsang ba'i mchog//rtog ge kun gyis mi
7a1(rgyud): @#//shes par//thams cad khyod la bshad par bya//yid gcig bsdus la mnyan par gyis//sems ni rgya ma drug bcu las//'das nas byang chub nyid kyi phyir//bsod nams ches
7a2(rgyud): 'byung snying po ste//byang chub 'byung ba skye bar 'gyur//nam mkha' lta bur dpag med cing//gos pa med do ci bzhin bya//chos kun gyis ni mi skul te//bzod nas zhi zhing mtshan nyid med//dpag
7a3(rgyud): med ye shes kyis bsgrubs pa//yang dag rdzogs sangs rgyas ston po//mchod pa dang rtsod pas bsgrubs pa'i phyir//byang chub par ni dngos po 'dod//gsang ba'i bdag po//de la 'khor
7a4(rgyud): ba thog ma med pa na byis ba so so'i skye bo rnams dang dang yar 'dzin pa la mngon par zhes pas/bdag gis khyad par grangs med par rab tu rtags te/gsang ba'i bdag po//del bdag gi
7a5(rgyud): ngo bo nyid la ma spyod par//bdag gzhis dang 'dzin pa kun nas slad do//gzhan dag ni dus las 'byung ba dang/'dzin pos sprul pa dang/nges par 'byung ba dang/'di lta ste dbang
7a6(rgyud): phyug dang 'byin pa po dang/dus dang rtso bo dang/byed pa po dang/byed pa pha ma yin pa dang/rnal 'byor dang /tshad ma dang/dag pha dang /ma dag pa dang/nang gi bdag dang/skyes bu dang/khyab
7a7(rgyud): dag dang/srog dang/gang zag dang/rnam par shes pa dang/kun gzhi dang/shes pa dang/mthong ba po dang/gzung ba dang/'dzin pa dang/shes po dang/shes bya dang/khong du chud par bya ba
7a8(rgyud): dang/shes las skyes pa dang/shed bu dang/brjod par bya ba dang/brjod par bya ba ma yin par rtog ste/gsang ba'i bdag po/bdag gi khyad par de lta bu lasogs pa'i rnal 'byor pa dag gis
7b1(rgyud): thar par 'dod pa'i sngon rtags pa yin no//gsang ba po'i bdag po gzhan yang byis pa so so'i skye bo phyugs dang mtshungs pa rnams las rgya lam na re 'ga' zhig las chos kyi 'du shes 'byung ba ni 
7b2(rgyud): 'di lta ste snyung bar gnas pa la gnas par bya'o//zhes de tsam zhig sems la bya ste mgu bar skyed nas/phyi phyi zhing de nyid la goms par byed de/gsang ba po'i bdag po de ni dge ba'i las skye ba'i sa
7b3(rgyud): bon lta bu ste/sems dang po'o//de rgyu de nyid kyi mtshan ma dang bcas pa'i nyi ma de dang/de dag la pha ma dang /bu dang bu mo dang/mdza' gshes dang/nye du dag la sbyin pa byin par byed de/'di
7b4(rgyud): ni nyu gu lta bu ste/gnyis pa'o//gzhan yang sbyin pa de nyid mi shes pa dag la yang sbyin par byed de/'di ni sngon bu lta bu ste gsum pa'o//gzhan yang sbyin pa'i de nyid snad du rung ba yongs su
7b5(rgyud): tshol zhing sbyin par byed de/'di na lo ma lta bu ste bzhi pa'o//gzhan yang sbyin po de nyid bla ma dang/rol mo mkhan la dga' bzhin du sbyin par byed de//'di ni ma log lta bu ste
7b6(rgyud): lnga pa'o//gzhan yang byin pa de nyid yid dga' ba dag la sbyin par byed de/'di 'bras lta bu ste drug pa'o//gsang ba po'i bdag po gzhan yang mtho ris su skye bar bya ba'i phyir tshul khrims
7b7(rgyud): srung ste/'di ni gsos su gyur pa lta bu ste bdun pa'o//gsang ba'i bdag po gzhan yang sems de lta bu nyid kyis 'khor bar rgya ba dang/dge ba'i gshes gnyen la la zhig las/tshig 'di skad
7b8(rgyud): ces thos te lha 'di dag ni lha chen te thams cad la ni bde bar ster/de la khyod kyis gus mchod dang/thams cad 'byor pa nyid du 'gyur//de dag kyang ni 'di lta ste//dbang phyug dang ni tshangs pa dang
8a1(rgyud): //de bzhin khyab 'jug bde byed dang//drag po skye ba byed de bzhin du//nyi ma zla ba chu lha nyid//lus ngag gnod sbyin rgya byin dang//mig mi bzang dang sna tshogs 'byung//gshin rjes dus
8a2(rgyud): mtshan mya ngan 'das//'jig rten rtso zhes bya ba dang//med du rnam par 'dud pa'i bu//de bzhin la mo dka' thub zlog//pad ma dang ni 'jug pa'i klu//nor ldan dang ni dung zhes bya//kar po ka rta pad
8a3(rgyud): ma che//rigs can dang ni gdengs ka mang//rtag tu mtha' yas theg ma'i lha//de bzhin lha dang drang srong mchog//rig byed skyes bu yin//mkhas pas mchod pa gyis shig ces//de las de 'dra'i tshig
8a4(rgyud): thos nas//yid ni yang dag mgu 'gyur te//de tshe rab tu gus ldan pas//de dag la ni nan tan byed//gsang ba po'i bdag po 'di ni byas pa so so'i skye bo 'khor ba na kun tu 'khyams pa
8a5(rgyud): rnams kyi dbugs 'byin pa'i mchog yin te//'di ni stobs su gyur pa ste//sems brgyad par rgyal ba rnams kyis bshad do//gsang ba po'i bdag pa//gzhan yang khyad par
8a6(rgyud): rtogs ci skad du yongasu sgrags pa'i khyad par de dang/de dag la khyad par du gnas pa rnams la/'ba' zhig pa rnams kyi don du blo skye zhing de yang yod do yod do zhes ston pa nyid kho na la dpos por ses te
8a7(rgyud): gsang ba po'i bdag po//de dag gis ni stong pa nyid du rab tu mi shes so//stong pa nyid ni dngos po yang ma yin/dngos po med pa yang ma yin/yod pa yang ma yin gnyi ka ma yin te/de la rtog pa
8a8(rgyud): 'am mi rtog pa yang med na/de la ci ltar stong pa zhes rtag par bya/gang dag stong pa nyid mi shes pas/de dag mya ngan 'das mi shes//de phyir dngos dang dngos med pa//'jig pas stong nyid shes par bya
8b1(rgyud): de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la phyag na rdo rjes gsol ba btab pa/bcom ldan 'das sems kyi khyad par de dag bshad du gsol//de skad ces gsol ba dang/bcom ldan 'das kyis gsang ba po'i
8b2(rgyud): bdag po phyag na rdo rje la bka' stsal pa/gsang ba po'i bdag po//sems kyi khyad par gyi mtshan nyid nyon cig/gsang ba'i bdag po de la sems 'di ni 'dod chags kyi sems dang/zhe sdang gi
8b3(rgyud): sems dang/byams pa'i sems dang/gti mug gi sems dang/shes rab kyi sems dang/gtan la phab pa'i sems dang/the tsom gi sems dang/mun par gyur pa'i sems dang/snang bar gyur
8b4(rgyud): pa'i sems dang/bsdud pa'i sems dang/'thab pa'i sems dang/rtsod pa'i sems dang/mi rtsod pa'i sems dang/lha'i sems dang/lha ma yin gyi sems dang/klu'i sems
8b5(rgyud): dang;;mi'i sems dang/bud med kyi sems dang/dbang phyug gi sems dang/tshong ba'i sems dang/zhing pa'i sems dang/chu bo'i sems dang/lteng ga'i sems dang/khron pa'i
8b6(rgyud): sems dang/kun tu srung ba'i sems dang/ser sna'i sems dang/khyi'i sems dang/byi lo'i sems dang/namakha' lding gi sems dang/byi ba'i sems dang/klu'i sems dang/gar gyi seMs dang/
8b7(rgyud): sil snyan gyi sems dang/khyim gyi sems dang/seng ge'i seMs dang/'ug pa'i sems dang/bya rog gi sems dang/srin po'i sems dang/tsher ma'i sems dang/sa 'og gi sems dang;rlung gi
8b8(rgyud): sems dang/chu'i sems dang/me'i sems dang/'dam gyi sems dang/rnyog pa'i sems dang/mtshon tshe'i sems dang/shing leb gi sems dang/'khrul pa'i sems dang/dug gi sems dang
9a1(rgyud): @#//zhags pa'i sems dang /lcags sgrog gi sems dang /sprin gyi sems dang /zhing gi sems dang /lan tsha'i sems dang /spu gri'i sems dang /ri rab lta bu'i sems dang /rgya mtsho'i 
9a2(rgyud): sems dang /skye bar 'gyur ba'i sems so//phug lta bu'i sems dang /skye bar 'gyurabar sems so//gsang ba po'i bdag po/de la 'dod chags kyi sems gang zhe na/chags pa can chos kun 
9a3(rgyud): tu ston pa'o//'dod chags dang bral ba'i sems gang zhe na/'dod chags dang bral ba chos kun ston pa'o//zhe sdang gi sems gang zhe na/sdang bas chos kun tu ston pa'o//byams pa'i seMs 
9a4(rgyud): gang zhe na/byams pa dang ldan pa'i chos ston pa'o//gti mug gi sems gang zhe na/gang mi spyod par chos ston pa'o/shes rab kyi sems gang zhe na/khyad par las khyad par gzhan
9a5(rgyud): rtogs par bya ste chos ston pa'o//gtan la phab pa'i sems gang zhe na/bka' dang bla ma'i tshig bzhin byed pa nyid do//the tsom gyi sems gang zhe na/ci ltar yongs su bzung ba nges
9a6(rgyud): par dag la so nyi sten pa nyid do//mun par gyur pa'i sems gang zhe na/som nyi med pa la som nyir 'du shes pa'o//snang bar gyur pa'i sems gang zhes na/so nyi med ma la so nyi med par sten
9a7(rgyud): pa'o//bsdud pa'i sems gang zhe na/du ma gcig tu sten pa'o//'thab pa'i sems gang zhe na /phan tsun rtsod pa nyid do//rtsod pa'i sems gang zhe na/bdag nyid dang bdag nyid du rtsod pa'i 
9a8(rgyud): mi rtsod pa'i sems gang zhe na/mnyan par mtshungs par lta ba'o//lha'i sems gang zhe na/yid kyis bdag nyid la rlom sems su byed pa'o//lha ma yin gyi sems gang zhe na/gang dag 'khor
9b1(rgyud): pa nyid la smod pa'o//klu'i sems gang zhe na/gang long spyod chen po nyid du bya ba'i phyir sems pa'o//mi I shes I gang zhe na/de nyid kyi don du sems pa'o//bud med kyi sems gang zhe na/
9b2(rgyud): 'dod pa kho na'i phyir chos sten pa'o//dbang phyug gi sems gang zhe na/gang bdag gis thams cad bsam po Sma mos I byed pa'o//chod pa'i sems gang zhe na/ I I par byas te rnam par
9b3(rgyud): spel nas phyis sten pa'o//zhing pa'i sems gang zhe na/gang thos pa rnam pa du ma tshol zhing phyis sten pa'o//chu bo'i sems gang zhe na/gang mtha' gnyis la ltos shed sten pa'o//I
9b4(rgyud): sems gang zhe na/gang sred la sten pa'o//khron pa'i sems gang zhe na/gang (ni)zab pa la zab par sten pa'o//kun tu bsrung ba'i sems gang zhe na/de nyid bden gyi gzhan I
9b5(rgyud): mug go snyam du sems pa'o//ser sna'i sems gang zhe na/bdag nyid sten cing gzhan la mi ster ba'o//byi la'i sems gang zhe na/gang mched pa la ltos te sten pa'i 
9b6(rgyud): khyi'i sems gang zhe na/gang chung ngus kyang mgu par gyur pa'o//namakha' lding gi sems gang zhe na/gangs phyogs 'dzin pas chos la sten pa'o//byi ba'i sems gang zhe na/'ching pa thams cad
9b7(rgyud): bcad pa'i phyir sems pa'o//klu'i sems gang zhe na/bdag gi dbyangs sna tshogs kyi klus sems can rnams bzung bar bya'o snyam pa'o//gar gyi sems gang zhe na/bdag gi rdzu 'phrul sna tshogs kyiI
9b8(rgyud): gzhan dag yang dag par 'dzin du bzhug gau snyam du bsam pa'o//sil snyan gyi sems gang zhe na/bad ga gis chos kyi rnga gdung I Ipa'o//khyim pa'i sems gang zhe na/badI ga lus kun tu srung
10a1(rgyud): /ba'i phyir sten pa'o//seng ge'i sems gang zhe na/gang thams cad zil gyis gnon pas sten pa'o//'ug pa'i sems gang zhe na/gang mtshan mo sems pa'o//bya rog gi sems gang
10a2(rgyud): gang thams cad du skrag par sems pa'o//srin po'i sems gang zhe na/dge bas mi dge ba rnam pas sgrub pa'o//tsher ma'i sems gang zhe na/gang thoms cad 'gyod pa sems pa'o//sa 'og gi seMs
10a3(rgyud): gang zhe na/sa'i 'og gi don du sems pa'o//rlung gi sems gang zhe na/gang la thams cad du thams cad sgrub pa'o//chu'i sems gang zhe na/gang mi dge ba thams bkru ba'i phyir bstan pa'o//me'i
10a4(rgyud): sems gang zhe na/gang dka' thub nyid kyis gdung bar byed pa'o//'dam gyi sems gang zhe na/gang bdag gi nyes pa gzhan la skud pa'o//rnyog pa'i sems gang zhe na/gang pha rol gyi nyes pa
10a5(rgyud): 'dzin pas sems la sa rnyog pa bskyed pa'o//1 tshon tse'i sems gang zhe na/gang de la chags pa nyid du sten pa'o//shing leb kyi sems gang zhe na/gang dge ba'i gzhan sos
10a6(rgyud): pas sten pa'o//nor pa'i sems gang zhe na/gzhan zung zhing gzhan khor sems pa'o//dug gi sems gang zhe na/gangs shin du dngos po med pa nyid sems pa'o//zhags pa'i sems gang zhe na
10a7(rgyud): gang thaMs cad du bdag nyid bcings shing 'dug go//lcags sgrog gi seMs gang zhe na/gang rkang pa gnyis la 'dug ste sems pa'o//srin po'i sems gang zhe na/gang chu dag la seMs pa'o//zhing gi seMs
10a8(rgyud): gang zhe na/rang gi las nyid yongasu sbyong bar byed pa'o//lan tsha'i seMs gang zhe na/gang zhig gang gis seMs dpar byed pa de la sems pa'o//spu gri'i seMs gang zhe na/gang rab tu byung ba tsam la rten pa'o//
10b1(rgyud): ri rab lta bu'i sems gang zhe na/gang mthong bar gyur nas seMs pa'o//rgya mtsho lta bu'i seMs gang zhe na/gang rang gi lus nyid la mchog tu 'dzin cing 'dug pa'o//phug lta bu'i seMs gang zhe na /gang sngar
10b2(rgyud): nyes par gyur la phyis de nyid kyis 'gyur bar ston pa'o//de la skye bar 'gyur ba'i sems gang zhe na/gang seMs 'du byed gang kho na dang/skyes pa de kho na dang lhan cig tu 'gro ba'o//gsang ba po'i bdag pa de
10b3(rgyud): ltar na gcig gnyis gsum bzhi lnga gnyisu sgyur ba byas pas/'jig rten pa'i seMs brgya drug bcu skal pa gsum gyis 'das nas/'jig rten las 'das pa'i seMs skye ste/'di lta ste 'di ni phung po tsam
10b4(rgyud): nyid do//bdag ni nam yang yod ma yin//dbang po'i grong khyer khams rnams kyis//'jig rten don med rnam par nyams//yan lag bcu gnyis rten 'brel 'di//nyon mongs las kyi kun
10b5(rgyud): na slad//ma rig sa bon las byung ba//byed pa la swogs rnam par spangs//de ltar zhi zhing shin du zab//rtog ge kun gyis shes par dka'//nyes pa thaMs cad rnam spangs pa//sangs rgyas
10b6(rgyud): rnaMs kyis sngon bstan pa'o//gsang ba po'i bdag po//de la 'jig rten las 'das pa'i sis la gnas pa rnaMs la yang phung po la yod do snyam pa'i blo nye bar skye'o//nam phung po dag la 'dod chags dang bral
10b7(rgyud): bar bya ba de'i tshe/lbu ba dang chu'i chu bur dang/sgyu ma dang smig rgyu dang mtshungs par 'jigs pas rnam par grol bar 'gyur te/'di lta ste phung po dang /khams dang skye mched dang/gzung ba dang 'dzin pa
10b8(rgyud): rnam par ma nges pa/chos nyid kyi rang gi ngo bo zhi ba'i dbyings rtogs par 'gyur ro//gsang ba po'i bdag pa//'jig rten las 'das pa'i seMs phyin ci log dang/phyin ci log ma yin pa'i seMs kyi khyad par
12a1(rgyud): //rten pa yongasu gtong ba de'i tshe/bdag med pas dbugs phyin pa yin no//nam phung po rnaMs spangs nas/chos kyi dmigs pa la gnas pa de'i tshe/chos kyi dbugs phyin pa yin no//
12a2(rgyud): nam chos rnaMs spangs nas mi skye ba la gnas pa de'i tshe/chos la bdag med pas dbugs phyinapa yin no//nam phung po dang/khams dang/skye mched dang/bzung ba dang /'dzin pa dang/bdag mgon
12a3(rgyud): po khyab bdag khyod kyis ni ses mtshan nyid bstan lags na/dbugs 'byin rnam pa du thob nas/mchog skyes de dag dbugs 'byin 'gyur//de skad ces gsol nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad
12a4(rgyud): chen po yis/de tshe phyag na rdo rje las yid bsdus 'di ni nyon ces gsungs/gsang ba po'i bdag po/de la nam byis pa so so'i skye bo dag mi dge ba'i las spangs te/dge ba'i las byed pa de'i
12a5(rgyud): tshe dge bas dbugs phyin pa yin no //nam phung po yongasu bzung bar bya ste/rang gi ngo bo nyid la spyad pa nas/bdag la rten pa yongasu gtong ba de'i tshe/bdag med pas dbu dang
12a6(rgyud): srog laswogs pa dang chos dang/seMs bskyed pa dag kyang stong pa nyid kyi ngo bo nyid du yang ngo bo nyid med do//zhes la stong pa'i nyid ye shes de'i tshe/chos thaMs cad kyi ngo bo nyid kyis dbugs phyin
12a7(rgyud): pa yin no//gsang ba po'i bdag po//de'i tshe byang chub seMs dpa' gsang sngags kyi sgo nas/byang chub seMs dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa bzhi bcus rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba rnaMs sgoms te/gsang sngags kyi
12a8(rgyud): tshul rtogs par khong du chud par 'gyur ro//bcu gang zhe na 'di lta ste/sgyu ma dang/smig rgyu dang/rmi lam dang/gzugs rnyan dang/dri za'i grong khyer dang/brag cha dang/chu zla dang /chu'i chu bur dang/'khrul
12b1(rgyud): pa dang 'gal me'i 'khor lo lta bur rnam par sgom mo//gsang ba po'i bdag po//de la byang chub seMs dpa' gsang sngags kyi sgo nas/byang chub seMs dpa'i spyad pa spyod pas/'di ltar so sor rtag par bya ste/
12b2(rgyud): de la sgyu ma gang zhe na 'di lta ste/sman dang sngags kyi sbyor ba byed pa dag gi sbyor pa las/dngos po rnam pa tha dad pa sna tshogs mig dang yid 'phrog pa dang/sngon ma mthong ba dag kyang ston te/de dag ni phan tshun
12b3(rgyud): phyogs bcur 'gro ba dang 'ong ba med do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/sgyu ma ni rang bzhin gyis stong pa'i phyir te/de bzhin du gsang sngags sbyor ba byed pa de dag gis sbyor ba las thaMs cad 'byung ngo//gsang ba
12b4(rgyud): po'i bdag po//gzhan yang smig rgyu ni rang bzhin gyis stong ba ste/de yang 'du shes kyis tha snyad du 'dogs te tha snyad kyang sgrub par byed do//de bzhin du 'du shes 'di yang gsang sngagaso/
12b5(rgyud): zhes tha snyad 'dogs shing tha snyad kyang sgrub par byedado//gsang ba po'i bdag po//gzhan yangabadag nyid nyin gcig dang/thang gcig dang/skad gcig dang/yud tsam dang lo lasogs
12b6(rgyud): par 'dug cing bde ba dang/sdug bsngal mang po myong ba mthong la /sad na ci yang ma mthong ba de bzhin du/gsang sngags kyi tshul 'di yang rmi lam lta bu khong du chud par bya'o//gsang ba po'i bdag po
12b7(rgyud): gzhan yang gsang sngags kyi dngos grub 'byung ba ni/gzugs rnyan lta bur rig par bya ste/dper na rdong dang me long la rten nas gzhan gyi gzugs rnyan 'byung ba ltar/gsang sngags kyi dngos grub kyang
12b8(rgyud): de dang 'dra bar rig par bya'o//gsang ba po' bdag po gzhan yang grub pa'i grub gnas ni/dri za'i grong khyer lta bur rig par bya'o//gsang pa po'i bdag po gzhan yang gsang sngags kyis sgrub pa'i sgra ni//
13a1(rgyud): @#//brag cha lta bur rig par byaste//dper na sgra la rten nas brag cha lta bur gsang sngags kyi tshul yang de dang 'dra bar rig par bya'o//gsang ba po'i bdag po gzhan yang zla ba shar na;chu'i
13a2(rgyud): nang na gzugs rnyan mthong nas chu zla zhes brjod pa de bzhin du/sngags pa de nyid du chu zla bzhin du rigs sngags 'chang zhes brjod do//gsang ba po'i bdag pa gzhan yang ji ltar char phab na;chu'i chu
13a3(rgyud): bur rnaMs 'byung ba de bzhin du gsang sngags grub pa'i sprul pa sna tshogs pa 'dod pa de/gsang ba po'i bdag po gzhal yang seMs med cing srog dang ni byed pa po yang med pa ste/dngos po thaMs cad stong pa
13a4(rgyud): la 'khrul pa kho nar snang bar zad/gsang ba po'i bdag po gzhan yang ji ltar 'ga' zhig bdag nyid kyis/lag na 'gal me thogs nas ni//skor na 'khor lo ltar snang ba//de bzhin stong
13a5(rgyud): pa dag las 'byung//gsang ba po'i bdag po'adi ni theg pa chen po'i gzhi/mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i bdag po nges pa'i gzhi/yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas pa'i
13a6(rgyud): gzhi;mthar gyis theg pa chen po la 'dzeg pa'i gzhi ste/ye shes sna tshogs cher 'byung ba//nor gyi dbyig 'di shes na ni//ji bzhin yang dag rab tu shes//bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad
13a7(rgyud): chen po mngon par byang chub pa/rnam par sprul pa'i byin gyis rlob pa las seMs kyi khyad par rim pa bye ba'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das la gsang ba'i bdag po phyag na rdo rjes 'di skad ces gsol to;
13a8(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das nyid kyis mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa'i chos kyi dbyings bsam gyis ma khyab pa/seMs can gyi sa las yang dag par 'das pa/bcom ldan 'das kyis thabs dang tshul sna tshog+sa
13b1(rgyud): dag gis;bsam pa dang mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du seMs can rnams la bstan pa ngo mtshar lagaso//bcom ldan 'das yang gsang sngags spyod pa'i tshul/snying rje chen po'i snying po 'byung ba/dkyil 'khor
13b2(rgyud): gyi rgyal po chen po yongasu rdzogs par bgyi ba'i slad du bshad du gsol//de ma 'ongs pa'i dus na/seMs can mang po dag gi don dang/phan pa dang bde bar 'gyur lagaso//de nas bcom ldan 'das
13b3(rgyud): rnam par snang mdzad kyis/thaMs cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor la gzigs nas/gsang ba po'i bdag po phyag na rdo rje la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//lag na rdo rje dkyil 'khor spyad pI
13b4(rgyud): tshul bstan pa'i tshul gyis rnam grangs thaMs cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes yongasu rdzogs par byed pa nyon cig/de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad chos kyi dbyings kyi dgongs pa ma rdzogs shing
13b5(rgyud): seMs can gyi khams ma lus sgral bar dam bca' ba mdzad pas/de bzhin gshegs pa thaMs cad dang thabs gcig tu yang dag par mos nas/snying rje chen po'i snying po 'byung ba
13b6(rgyud): zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugaso//bcom ldan 'das snyoMs par zhugs ma thag tu/sku'i yan lag thaMs cad nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i seMs dang po skyed pa nas bzung ste sa bcu'i
13b7(rgyud): bar du seMs can phan pa'i don mdzad pa rnaMs byung ngo//byung nas phyogs pas kun tu skor te slar log nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi sku'i nang rang gi gnas rnamasu zhugaso//de nas yang bcom ldan
13b8(rgyud): 'das kyis byang chub seMs dpa' phyag na rdo rje la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//lag na rdo rje dkyil 'khor rnam par bkod pa nyon cig/de shig dang po slob dpon yid byang chub kyi seMs la zhug+sa pa/
14a1(rgyud): @#//shes rab dang ldan pa brtan pa snying rje can sgyu rtsal dag la mkhas pa/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul brtag du mkhas pa/thob pa gsum gyi khyad par gsang 
14a2(rgyud): sngags kyi de kho na nyid kyi don la mkhas pa/bsam pa shes pa sangs rgyas dang byang chub seMs dpa' rnams la dang po/dkyil 'khor 'bri ba'i las byed par gnang zhing dbang skur ba/rigs btsun
14a3(rgyud): gsang sngags spyad pa/mngon par brtson zhing gtan la bab sbyor la mkhas shing zungs thub la/byang chub seMs la rnam par gnas//gsang dang po'i bdag po slob dpon de lta bu'i rgyal ba'i sras
14a4(rgyud): yin te;sangs rgyas dang byang chub sesaM dpa' rnaMs kyis rab tu sngagaso//gsang ba pa'i bdag po de la song na gzhan gyis gsol ba btab kyang rung/yang na bdag gi seMs can
14a5(rgyud): dag;;snod du rung zhes nyes bral ba//mos mang bstan la dad pa can//gzhan la phan dga' thong nas ni//de lta bu'i slob ma de//shes nas slob dpon bdag nyid kyis//ma gsol
14a6(rgyud): bar ni de bos te//phan 'dod phyir ni 'di skad bya//khyod ni tshul chen snod yin gyis//bu tshur theg pa chen po yi//gsang sngags spyod tshul cho ga ni//khyad la yang dag bstan par bya//rdzogs pa'i
14a7(rgyud): sangs rgyas gang 'das te//de bzhin gang dag ma byon dang//da lta byung ba'i mgon po rnaMs//'gro la phan phyir bzhugs pa dag//de dag kun gyis gsang sngags kyi//cho ga bzang po 'di mkhyen nas/
14a8(rgyud): dpa' bos byang chub shing drung du//thaMs cad mkhyen pa tshad med snyed//gsang sngags sbyor ba thaMs cad mnyam med de//shakyA seng ge skyob pa yis//bdud sde shin du mi zad pa//dpung chen dag kyang
14b1(rgyud): de yis bcom//de bas kun mkhyen thob bya'i phyir//bu yis blo gros 'di gyis shig//snying rjes gyur pa'i yid kyis ni//de ltar lan sang gzengs stod de//slob ma de rtan shes nas ni//sa gzhi leg+sa
14b2(rgyud): par yongs rtag bya//me tog 'bras bur ldan pa'i ri//gtsang zhig yid 'ong chu'i gnas//de dag dkyil 'khor bri ba yi//las la sangs rgyas kun gyi sngags//gang na 'bab chu mang po dag//ngang pa gzhan
14b3(rgyud): gyis rgyan yod pa//der ni sngags pa blo ldan gyis//snying rje 'byung ba'i dkyil 'khor bri//sangs rgyas rnaMs dang rang mgon dang//de bzhin rgyal ba'i nyan thos kyis//sa phyogs gang dag snyen pa ni//de dag
14b4(rgyud): thub pas rtag tu sngags//phyogs gzhan rnam pa sna tshogs pa||lha khar kun dka' bzang po dang//khyim dang yang thog nyaMs dga' dang//tshe'u dang skyed mos chal mdzes dang//mchod rten grang
14b5(rgyud): gang ba lang lhas//'bab chu 'dus pa'i mdo dag dang//lha'i gnas rnaMs thams cad dang//de bzhin khang pa stong par yang//slob ma dag la phan 'dod phyir//nan tan du ni dkyil
14b6(rgyud): 'khor bri//yang na yid du 'ong ba'i sa//gang yang rung bar bri bar bya//gsang ba po'i bdag po de las gzhi yongasu sbyang bar bya ba ni/rdo dang bong ba dang/kyo mo dang gseg ma med cing/skra dang phug
14b7(rgyud): ma dang;sol ba dang/thal ba dang/tsher ma dang/rus pa dang /sdong bum dang/zug rngu gsal ba dang/grog spur dang/sprin bu dang /srog chags med cing nyes pa thaMs cad spangs pa'i gnas ni lha rnaMs
14b8(rgyud): la rab tu shes pa'o//de la ches grangs dang rgyu skar dang/gza' dang byed pa dang/yud tsam bzang po'i mtshan ma dang/ldan pa'i nyi ma snga gro'i dus kyi tshe ltas bzang pos mtshon pa la/de bzhin gsheg+sa
15a1(rgyud): @#//pa thams cad la phyag byas te/sa'i lha mo rtan ma'i tshigasu bcad pa gnyis sgrags par bya'o//sangs rgyas skyob pa thaMs cad kyi//spyod pa'i tshul gyi khyad par ni//sa dang pha rol phyin dag la//
15a2(rgyud): lha mo khyod ni dbang du gyur//shakyA seng ge skyob pa yis//ji ltar bdud kyi sde bcom pa//de bzhin bdag gis bdud las ni//rgyal bar byas te dkyil 'khor bri//sa la lag pas reg bya zhes//de ltar
15a3(rgyud): lan mang sprad nas ni//pus mo gnyis ni der btsugs nas//me tog spos kyis mchod par bya//mchod nas sngags pas de nas ni//de bzhin gshegs la phyag 'tshal te//de nas cho ga 'di dag gis//
15a4(rgyud): mkhas pas sa gzhi sbyang bar bya//de nas phyag na rdo rjes bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la gtugs te/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//sangs rgyas mtshan ma mi mnga' ste//chos
15a5(rgyud): kyi sku la rnam par zhugs//mtshan med 'dus ma byas pa yi//mtshungs pa med pa'i chos ston na//dpa' bo chen po ci slad du//mtshan mar bcas pa'i cho ga 'dis//gsang sngags spyad
15a6(rgyud): la bstan par mdzad//'di ni chos nyid tshul ma lags//de skad gsol nas bcom ldan 'das//rnam par snang mdzad chen po yis//de tshe phyag na rdo rje la//chos kyi mtshan nyid 'di nyon gsungs//chos
15a7(rgyud): ni rtog dang bral ba ste//kun rtog thaMs cad spangs pa yin//de la seMs dang seMs byung gi//rtog pa rab tu spong bar 'gyur//nga yis chos mngon sangs rgyas pa//de ni mkhas las byung ba ste//log par rtog
15a8(rgyud): pa'i spyod yul ba//byis pa rnaMs kyis mi shes so//yul dang mtshan ma laswogs pa//mun gyis sgribs pa rnaMs 'dod te//de dag gzud par bya ba'i phyir//cho ga 'di yang brjod pa yin//yul med dus
15b1(rgyud): kyang med pa ste//bya ba med cing byed pa med//chos rnaMs kun la yod med do//chos rnaMs nyi tshe 'ga' zhig go//gsang ba po'i bdag po//'on kyang seMs can dag ni blo zhan cing//bya ba tsam gyis rmongs pa
15b2(rgyud): dag//dngos po'i don la chags pa rnaMs//ma 'ongs dus na 'byung bar 'gyur//'di lta bu'i tshul ni mi shes pas//yul dang dus dang bya ba las//dge dang mi dge'i mtshan ma yis//rmongs pa'i 'bras
15b3(rgyud): bu 'dod pa de//seMs dpa' chen po de dag phyir//cho ga 'di ni brjod pa yin//gsang ba po'i bdag po de la ci skad bstan pa'i gnas gang yang rung bar sa gzhi rab tu mthas par cags te/ba'i lji ba
15b4(rgyud): sa la ma lhung ba dang/ba'i gcin du sres pas byug go//de la sngags 'dis gsang rtor bya ste sngagaso////na ma sa man ta bu nAn/a pra ti sa me ga g+ha na/sa ma ta nu ga te/pra tri ti/bi shud+d+he
15b5(rgyud): d+ha rma d+ha du/bi sho d+ha ni swA hA////de'i 'og tu sngags pa ni langs nas de'i dbus su//pad ma dkar po'i bdan la bzhugs/thor tshugs dang ni u rgyan 'chang//'od zer kha dog sna tshogs
15b6(rgyud): pas//kun nas yongasu skor bar bsam//de nas rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas bzhi//blo dang ldan pas phyogasu bsam//de bzhin gshegs pa rin chen tog//nyi ma shar ka lta bu bsam//lho ru rgyal
15b7(rgyud): ba dpa' bo ni//me tog kun rgyas zhes bya ba//gser gyi kha dog 'bar bar bcas//nyes pa bcom pa'i ting 'dzin gnas//byang phyogasu ni mi 'khrugs pa//nad dang bral ba ting 'dzin gnas//nub phyog+sa
15b8(rgyud): 'od dpag med pa ste//rgyal ba lnga po 'di rnasaM ni//bsaMs te de nas sngags pa yi//rgyal ba'i gnas dbus 'dug nas su//mi g.yo bdag nyid chen po 'am//yang na 'jig rten gsum gyi rgyal//gsang
16a1(rgyud): //sngags don kun bsgrub pa yis//sa ni yongasu bzung bar bya//dbus su dkyil 'khor bzang po dag//tsan dan dag gis bya ba ni//dbang po gcig ni nga'i yin//gnyis pa skyob pa thaMs cad
16a2(rgyud): kyi'o;;gsum pa de dang mnyam bya ba//the tsom med par yum gyis yin//bzhi pa lag na pad ma ste//lnga pa phyag na rdo rje'i phyir//drug pa mi g.yo mgon gyi ste//ma zad 'og tu legs par bya//de bzhin
16a3(rgyud): gshegs rnaMs rjes dran nas//me tog spong la swogs phul te//mnyam par bzhag nas gus pa ru//chig tu 'di skad smra bar bya//mgon po thugs rje'i bdag nyid can//thaMs cad bdag la dgongasu gsol
16a4(rgyud): sras bcas sang gi gdugs 'das na//sa gzhi yongasu bzung bar mdzod//de skad kyis ni tshig smras nas//gsang sngags 'di ni brjod par bya//na ma sa man ta bu ta nAn/sarba ta thA g+ha tA shrA nA
16a5(rgyud): d+hi sh+tha te;a tsa le;bi ma le;smra ra ne;pra ti ti ba ri shud+d+he swA hA////de nas sngags pas langs nas su//snying rjer gyur pa'i yid kyis ni//nub kyi phyogasu gnas su//nang gi bdagi
16a6(rgyud): nyid rnam dag pas//byang chub seMs ni seMs byed cing//de nyid du ni nyal bar bya//de nas sngags pas rmi lam lta//byang chub seMs dpa' grags chen nam//yang na sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das//
16a7(rgyud): seMs can rnaMs la phan bya'i phyir//dkyil 'khor bri ba'i las rnaMs la//bdag nyid kun tu rtsol 'bad pa 'am//bdag nyid la ni 'di skad ces//seMs can chen po bu khyod legs//dkyil 'khor myur du
16a8(rgyud): bris shig ces//spro ba bskyed pa mthong yang rung//de nas de'i phyi de nyin//slob ma dad cing rigs btsun pa//de bzhin dkon mchog gsum la dad//zab mo yi ni blo dang ldan//spro ba che zhing tshul khrims
16b1(rgyud): ldan;;bzod dang ldan zhing ser sna med//dpa' la yid dam gtan pa ni//bcu 'am brgyad dam bdun nam lnga//gcig gnyis bzhi las lhag kyang rung //spyad mi dgos par gzung bar bya//de nas yang
16b2(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das la phyag na rdo rjes zhus pa//bcom ldan 'das dkyil 'khor 'di'i ming ci lags//ci'i slad du dkyil 'khor zhes bgyi//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//gsang be po'i bdag
16b3(rgyud): po;;dkyil 'khor 'di ni sangs rgyas 'byung ba ste//dkyil 'khor ni snying po zhes bya'o//'khor ni rdzogs pa ste//de'i gong na snying por gyur ba gzhan med pa//de nas dkyil 'khor zhes bya'o//gsang ba
16b4(rgyud): po'i bdag po;;'on kyang dkyil 'khor rgyas pa 'di ni;;seMs can gyi khams ma nges pa yongasu skyab pa'i phyir//snying rje chen po'i snying po 'byung ba zhes bya ba ste//de bzhin gshegs pa
16b5(rgyud): rnaMs kyis skal pa grangs med par//bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub yang dag par bsgrubs pa byin gyis rlabaso//gsang ba po'i bdag po//de lta bas na rnam
16b6(rgyud): grangs 'dis kyang //khyod kyis 'di ltar de bzhin gshegs pa rnaMs ni seMs can gcig gi phyir/byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa ma yin /gnyis kyi phyir ma yin gsum gyi phyir ma yin;
16b7(rgyud): 'on kyang thugs rje chen po'i dbang du gyur pa//bdag seMs can khams ma lus pa yongasu skyab pa'i phyir//byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas te//de dag gis seMs can gyi khams rnam
16b8(rgyud): pa du ma la bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du /seMs can rnaMs pa chos 'chad par rig par bya'o//gsang ba po'i bdag po//theg pa chen po la goms par ma byas pa rnaMs ni gsang sngags spyad pa'i tshul mthong
17a1(rgyud): @#//ngam thos na/seMs la dga' ba 'am dad pa cung zad kyang yang dag par mi skye yi/gsang ba po'i bdag po gang dag gis ston//theg pa chen gsang sngags spyad pa'i tshul sgo mtha' yas
17a2(rgyud): pa bsgrub pa la goms par byas pa de dag ni/rdo rje seMs dpa' yin te/de rnaMs nyid kyi don gyi phyir grangs kyi tshad 'di byas so//'on kyang slob dpon snying rje chen po dang ldan pa seMs can gyi khams ma
17a3(rgyud): lus par sgral bar yid dam bca' bar kho nar bya ste/des byang chub kyi seMs kyi rgyur 'gyur bar bya ba'i phyir//seMs can tshad med pa rnaMs yongasu bzung bar bya'o//de nas de dag bos la gsum la skyabasu
17a4(rgyud): btang bar yang bya'o//sdig pa mi bya ba'i rnams kyang sgo bar bya'o//de nas de dang la spos dang me tog laswogs pa sbyin no//de dag dus gsum du sgrib pa med pa'i ye shes kyi sdom pa yang /
17a5(rgyud): 'dzin du bzhug go//'dzin du bcug na sngags pa yis/de dag so shing bdag kyang sbyin//rtse gcig mnyam par gzhag pa yang //shar ram byang du kha ltas te//u dum b+ha ra a shad thA
17a6(rgyud): shing du bzang po srung byas shing //metog dri laswogs pa skus pa yi//sa shing dag ni 'chang bzhugs so//seMs can snod rung snod mi rung //bor ba nyid las rtag par bya//de nas lag pa g.yas
17a7(rgyud): g.yon la//skud pa 'dud pa gsum ldan pa//bdag nyid mnyam par bzhag pa yis//legs pa ru ni bcing bar bya//de ltar yongasu bzung ldan pa'i//slob ma nyes pa med rnaMs la//bsaMs la rtan
17a8(rgyud): par bya ba'i phyir//'di ltar spro ba bskyed par bya//bdag nyid chen po khyed rnaMs kyis//rnyed pa rab brnyed de ring rnyed//gang phyir rgyal ba sras bcas pa//bdag nyid chen po kun gyis ni//bstan pa
17b1(rgyud): 'di la khyed rnaMs kun//yongasu bzung ste skye bzhin pa//de phyir khyed rnaMs theg chen la//sa ni rab tu skyes par 'gyur//gal te rmi lam dag du ni//rtsug lag khang dang kun dga' bzang //khang pa bzang
17b2(rgyud): po sna tshogs dang//de bzhin du yang yang thog dang//ral gri yid bzhin nor bu dang//gdugs dang me tog sna tshogs dang//bud med bzang po gos dkar can//dug pa'i gnyen dang bu dag dang//
17b3(rgyud): klegs bam de bzhin bram ze dang//sangs rgyas dang ni rang sangs rgyas//de bzhin rgyal ba'i nyan thos dang//byang chub seMs dpa' bdag nyid che//gal te 'bras bu thob pa dang//mtsho dang rgya mtsho
17b4(rgyud): las rgal mthong//de bzhin du ni namakha' las//bkra shis yid du 'ong ba'i tshig//'dod pa'i 'bras bu 'byung ba drag//de la sogs pa'i rmi lam ni//bzang po zhes ni bya ba rtag//mkhas pas de
17b5(rgyud): las zlog pa yi//rmi lam ngan pa shes par bya//rtul shugs bzang po khyed rnaMs kyis//nang par thas cad nga la zlos//slob ma de dag rnaMs la ni//de skad sgo nas chos kyang bshad//
17b6(rgyud): theg pa chen po cher 'byung ba'i//lam mchog 'di ni dpal dang ldan//khyed rnaMs der ni dong bas na//rang 'byung skal pa chen po pa//'jig rten thaMs cad mkhyen pa yi//de bzhin gsheg+sa
17b7(rgyud): pa rnamasu 'gyur//yod dang med la rnam par 'das//namakha' bzhin du dri ma med//zab mo rtog ge thaMs cad kyis//rab tu mi rtog nas med pa//spros pa kun dang bral ba ste//spros pa rnams
17b8(rgyud): kyis rnam par spros//las dang bya ba laswogs bral//bden pa gnyis la phan gnas pa'i//theg pa rab kyi mchog 'di ni//tshul la gnas na thob par 'gyur//de nas bcom ldan 'das la rdo rje
18a1(rgyud): @#//'dzin spros pa med par gnas pas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das dus gsum du sgrib pa ma mchis pa'i ye shes kyi sdom pa gang la byang chub seMs dpa' rnaMs gnas na//
18a2(rgyud): sangs rgyas dang byang chub seMs dpa' rnasaM mnyes par 'gyur ba bshad du gsol//de skad ces gsol pa dang/bcom ldan 'das kyis rdo rje 'dzin spros pa med par gnang pa la 'di skad ces bka' stsal
18a3(rgyud): to;;rigs kyi bu lus dang ngag dang yid gcig tu 'jug cing/chos thaMs cad la brtsom pa med cing bya ba med pa'i sdom pa gang yin pa de//rigs kyi bu nyon cig//de gang zhe na/sangs rgyas dang
18a4(rgyud): byang chub seMs dpa' rnaM la bdag gi lus yongasu gtong ba gang yin pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/gang gis bdag gi lus yongasu gtongab des ni dngos po gsum btang ba yin te;dngos po
18a5(rgyud): gsum yongasu btang ba de yang gang zhe na/'di lta ste lus dang ngag dang yid do//rigs kyi bu de lta bus na byang chub seMs dpa' lus dang ngag dang yid gsum gyi sdom pa sdam par
18a6(rgyud): bya'o;;de ci'i phyir zhe na/byang chub seMs dpa' rnasaM ni lus dang ngag dang yid yongasu ma btang bar slab pa 'dzin par mi byed do//des na phyi de nyin bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad la phyag
18a7(rgyud): 'tshal te/bdag nyid rdo rje seMs dpar byin gyis rlabs la/bum pa spos chus bkang ba la 'jig rten gsum las rnam par rgyal bas mngon par sngags te/sgo dang por bzhag la mkhas pas
18a8(rgyud): skye bo kun la des gsang rtor bya'o//de nas seMs sbyar ba'i phyir spos chu gtsang ma blud do//de nas bcom ldan 'das la phyag na rdo rjes 'di skad ces gsol to//dkyil 'khor gyi ni slob dpon dag/
18b1(rgyud): gsang sngags bsgrub la 'bad pa ni//gang gi dus su dkyil 'khor du//seMs dpa' chen po byin rlob pa'i//dus dang tshod ni kun mkhyen pa//smra ba'i mchog gis bka' stsol cig//de skad
18b2(rgyud): ces gsol nas;bcom ldan 'das kyis rdo rje 'dzin la bka' stsal pa//dkyil 'khor don du gnyer ba la//rtag tu mtshan mo bri bar bya//lag na rdo rje de nas slob dpon gyis//sangs rgyas thaMs
18b3(rgyud): cad phyag byas la//mtshon sna lnga'i srad bu thogs te//bdag nyid bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad byin gyis rlabs la/shar du srad bu bri ba ni//lte ba'i thad kar bzung nas su//bar snang la ni
18b4(rgyud): bzung bar bya//de nas lho phyogs song gnas su//byang phyogasu ni bri bar bya//de nas srad bu gnyis pa ni//de bzhin gshegs pa rjesu dran cing //shar phyogs ltas te bri bar bya//
18b5(rgyud): de nas lho phyogs song nas ni//byang phyogasu ni bri bar bya//blo can de nas slas log nas//bden bral phyogasu gnasna ni//byang phyogasu ni bri bar bya//de nas g.yasu sgyur bar bya//
18b6(rgyud): sgyur te de ru 'dug nas nas ni//lho phyogasu ni bri bar bya//daM nas gnas te btang bar bya//me'i phyogasu gnas nas ni//sngags pas gsang sngags cho gar gnas//shar du srad bu bri bar bya//
18b7(rgyud): de nas de bzhin dbang ldan phyogs//song nas nub tu kha ltas te//rlung gi phyogs kyi bar du ni//byang phyogasu ni bri par bya//sngags pas yis ni de lta bur//kun nas gru bzhi lham par bya;;de nas
18b8(rgyud): dbus su zhugs nas su//de la cha ni gsum du rtag//sa rnaMs thaMs cad kun tu yang//cha gsum dag tu rtags nas ni//cha rnaMs kyang ni re re la//cha gsum du ni rab tu rtag//cha gcig gcig ni
19a1(rgyud): @#//skor ba yi//'khor bar byas te lhag ma yi//cha gnyis pa ni lha nyid kyi//gnas su nges par bya ba yin//blo dang ldan pas phyogs bzhi ru//so sor phyogs char sgo khyud ni//sdom
19a2(rgyud): rtson can gyis nan tan du//lha rnam gtso bo'i gnas su bya//de ltar rim pas legs phye ba//'di dag thaMs cad byas nas ni//dbus su pad ma dkar po'i mchog//de'i snying po zhes su grags;dkyil
19a3(rgyud): 'khor snying rje 'byung ba kun//des ni snying po can du 'gyur//de'i tshad kyang sor bcu drug//yan chad du ni bshad pa yin//'dab ma brgyad pa legs par rdzogs//ze bar ldan la shin du bzang//
19a4(rgyud): 'dab ma'i bar ni thaMs cad du//kun nas rdo rje bri bar bya/de'i snying po'i dbus su ni//rgyal mchog gtso bo gser gyi mdog//'bar bcas thor tshugs u rgyan can//'od zer dag gis kun nas skor;;
19a5(rgyud): mgon po nad dang bral ba ni//ting nge 'dzin du gnas pa bri//de'i shar du blo ldan gyis//sangs rgyas rnaMs kyi phyag rgya ni//gru gsum thaMs cad thad kar nyid//pad ma'i steng du khor yug tu
19a6(rgyud): 'od zer dkar pos yongs skor ba//rab tu sgrims te bri bar bya//de'i byang du mkhas rnaMs kyis//skyob pa dag gi kun gyi yum//gser gyi mdog can 'bar dang bcas//na bza' dag ni dkar po
19a7(rgyud): bsnaMs;;kun du rnam par snang mdzad pa//ting nge 'dzin du gnasapa bri//mgon po yin ni byang phyogasu //spyan ras gzigs dbang dpa' ni//pad ma dkar mo'i stan la 'dug//du dang kun da zla ba dang//
19a8(rgyud): dbu la 'od dpag med pa bzhugs//bzhin ni bag tsam 'dzum pa bri//de'i g.yasu lha mo ni//sgrol mar bcas pa chen mo de//dge zhing 'jigs pa sel ba ste//sngo sangs mo la sna tshogs gzugs;;bud med
19b1(rgyud): dar la bab pa'i tshe//thal sbyar metog ud pal thogs//'od zer dag gis kun skor cing//gos ni dkar po gyon pa bri//g.yon du lha mo khro gnyer can//sngags de'i phreng ba lag na sbyangs//mig gsum pa
19b2(rgyud): la thor tshugs can//kha dog 'dog ni dkar mo la//'od zer dkar po ser po dang//dmar po rnaMs kyis bskor bar bri//khro gnyer can nid nye bar bri//mthu chen lag na pad mo thogs//rtse ba chen po gos kar
19b3(rgyud): gyon;;'od zer dag gis bskor ba yi//dpa' bo yang dag bri bya ste//de'i pad ma kha ma bye//de'i drung du blo can gyis//rigs sngags lha mo grags ldan ma//gser gyi kha dog lta bu can//rgyan rnaMs kun
19b4(rgyud): gyis brgyan pa ste//lag pa g.yon pas pri yang ku'i//snyi ma yid 'ong 'dzin pa bri//sgrol ma yi ni nye 'khor du//blo dang ldan pas gos dkar mo//thor tshugs can la gos dkar gyon//lag na
19b5(rgyud): pad ma thogs pa bri//de'i 'og tu sngags pa yis//rigs sngags rgyal po stobs po che//kha dog nyi ma kar 'char kha bzhin//pad ma kar pos rnam par brgyan//'bar ba'i phreng can mi zad cing 
19b6(rgyud): spu dag seng ge ltar 'dug pa//spyan ras gzigs dbang blo ldan gyis//rta 'brin can zhis grags pa bri//sangs rgyas rnaMs kyis lho phyogasu//byang chub seMs dpa' skyob rnaMs kyis//phyag rgya yid bzhin nor bu zhes//
19b7(rgyud): grags pa skal pa chen po ces//re ba thaMs cad rdzogs byed pa//pad ma kar la 'dug par bri//mgon po rnam par snang mdzad kyi//lho'i phyogasu rdo rje 'dzin//re ba thaMs cad rdzogasabyed pa//pad ma dkar po 'dug par bri
19b8(rgyud): mgon po rnam par snang mtshad kyi//lho'i phyogasu rdo rja 'dzin//re thaMs cad skong byed pa//mdog ni pri yang ku lta bu//ljang ser ram ni ma rgad ltar//sngo sangs mgon po dbu rgyan can//seMs dpa' chen po'i
20a1(rgyud): /rgyan kun gyis//rnam par rgyan cing mtha' yas par//'jug pa rgya chen rdo rje bcas//'od zer dag gis bskor bar bri//de'i g.yasu lha mo ming//ma ma ki zhes bya ba de//rdo rje rgyan gyis brgyan
20a2(rgyud): pa ste;;lag pa rdo rje thogs pa bri//de'i 'og tu lha mo ni//rdo rje rtse mo stobs chen de//pho nya rnaMs kyis yongs bskor cing//bzhin du bag tsam 'dzum pa bris//g.yos rol du ni sngags pa yis//rdo rje lu gu rgyud
20a3(rgyud): ces pa//lag na rdo rje lu gu d+yud//rang gi pho nya rnaMs kyis bskor//pri yang ku ltar ljang ser mdog//rdo rje'i mtshan mas legs mtshan bri//lag na rdo rje'i 'og logasu//khro zla gtso bo zhes bya ba//dgegs chen
20a4(rgyud): dgra ni zhi byed pa//khro bo 'jig rten gsum du grags//mig gsum mche ba bzhi pa ste//kha dog dbyar gyi char sprin 'dra//rab tu rgod pa'i sgra yang 'byin//rdo rje'i rgyan gyis rnam par brgyan//'khor lo mtha'
20a5(rgyud): yas khro bo yi//dbang phyug rnaMs kyis yongs bskor ba//lag pa stong phrag brgya dag ni//mtshon cha sna tshogs thogs shing gdags/khro bo chen po 'di 'dra ba//pad ma'i steng du 'dug pa bri;de'i nub tu
20a6(rgyud): song nas ni//rdo rje 'dzin pa mang po dag//lag na mtshon cha sna tshogs thogs//kha dog sna tshogs stobs po che//'od zer dag gis yongs skor ba//seMs can kun don byed pa bri//gsang sngag+sa
20a7(rgyud): mgon po'i 'og logasu//bden bral gyi ni phyogs kyi char//mi g.yo zhes bya lag na ni//ral gri zhags pa legs par thogs//zur phud spyi bo'i g.yon du 'chang//rab tu rgyan cing mig cig g.yo//rang gi
20a8(rgyud): 'od kyi nang na gnas//khro dang bcas pa drag pa 'dug//bzhin ni khro gnyer rab tu gnyer//byis pa'i gzugs can mkhrang drag bri//bris nas blo ldan rlung phyogasu//song nas bcom ldan 'das kyi ni//khro bo 'jig
20b1(rgyud): rten gsum las ni//'bar ba'i phreng can mi zad pa//lag na rdo rje rtse bran brgyan//bka' lung nod par ltar 'dug cing//zad pa'i dus dang 'drar gnas pa//nan tan du ni bri bar bya//de ltar nang du bri ba ni bya//
20b2(rgyud): rgyas dang bcas par bshad pa yin//de nas sngags pa phyir phyung nas//nges par gnyis pa'i cha dag la//shar kyi phyogs kyi nang du ni//cha ni gnyisu myed pa la//sgo khyud kyi nang rol du//sngags pas rgyal ba
20b3(rgyud): shAkya thub;;gser gyi kha dog 'bar ba bcas//mtshan mchog sum bcu rtsa gnyis ldan//mgon po na bza' dur smigs bsnaMs//khyab bdag bsnam pa'i don mdzad pa//pad ma dkar po'i bdan la bzhugs//
20b4(rgyud): chos 'chad tshul du bzhugs pa bri//de'i g.yasu lha mo ni////sangs rgyas spyan zhes bya ba ni//lha mo cung zad 'dzum pa'i zhal//'od kyi dkyil 'khor 'dom gang ba//mtshungs med sku ni rab
20b5(rgyud): tu dang;;de ni shAkya thub pa'i yum//de'i g.yasu lha mo ni//re ba kun rdzogs byed pa bri//de nas sngags pas g.yon rol du//shAkya seng ge skyob pa yis/rigs sngags mdzod spu zhes grag+sa pa
20b6(rgyud): pad ma'i steng na 'dug na 'dug pa ste//dung zla kun d+ha lta bu'i mdog//lag na yid bzhin nor bu thogs//re ba kun rdzogs byed pa bre//de nas sngags pas g.yo na rol du//shAkya seng ge skyob payis;;rtsug
20b7(rgyud): tor gzi can brtson 'grus che//gdugs dkar po dang rgyal ba yi//rtsug tor rnam par rgyal nyid dang//gzi brjid phung po skal chen dang//rnam par mthor zhes rnam grags pa//lnga po yang dag bri bar bya;
20b8(rgyud): shAkya tog ni mthu chen gyi//rtsug tor lnga po 'di dag ni//gnas ni lnga rnaMs nyid du ni//rab tu sgrigs te bri bar bya//de'i byang du song nas su//gnas gtsang rnaMs ni bri bar bya//dbang phyug kun tu metog dang//
21a1(rgyud): //'od kyi phreng ba nyid dang ni//yid 'gyogs dang ni rnam grags te//de dag thasaM (ma)legs par bri//mdzod spu yi ni g.yas logasu//gtsug tor chen po cher 'byung dang//de bzhin gyen du 'byung
21a2(rgyud): nyid dang;;sgra dbyangs mtha' yas pa yang ste//gtsug tor gsum po kho na ni//sgrims te yang dag bri bar bya//gtsug tor lnga pa stobs chen rnaMs//dkar po ser po ser skyar bya//gsum po gzhan ni dkar
21a3(rgyud): po dang;;ser po dang ni dmar por bya//kun kyang 'od zer mang po can//rgyan rnaMs kun gyis brgyan pa yin//de nas smon lam stobs chen te//re ba thaMs cad rdzogs byed pa'o//de dag sngags pas me phyog+sa
21a4(rgyud): su;;me'i drang srong che rnaMs dang//bcas shing me'i nad na 'dug//thal ba'i gso ris gsum gyis mtshan//dpa' bo thaMs cad rab tu dmar//snying kha sum mdo ris mtshan can//me lce 'bar ba'i dbus
21a5(rgyud): su 'dug;;bgrad phreng kun gyis thogs par bri//de nas lho'i phyogs kyi char//gshin rje'i gtso bo lag pa na//dbyug pa thogs shing mahe zhon//kha dog dbyar gyi char sprin 'dra//ma mo
21a6(rgyud): rnaMs dang dus mtshan dang//'chi bdag rnaMs kyis bskor ba bri//bden bral phyogasu bden bral ni//lag na ral gri 'jigs pa bri//de nas kyang ni blo ldan pa//myur du byang gi phyogs song ste;;lha'i
21a7(rgyud): rgyal po ri rab la//'dug cing lha rnaMs kyis bskor ba//lag na rdo rje gcod pan can//rgyud kun gyis ni rgyan pa bri//de nas de'i shar du ni//chu lha lag na zhags thogs bri//de'i yang na shar phyogasu//nyi ma shing
21a8(rgyud): rta'i steng du bri//de'i g.yas dang g.yon du ni//rgyal dang rnam par rgyal ba bri//de'i g.yasu tshangs pa ni//gdong bzhi pa la thor tshugs can//A+oM zhes bya ba lhur len pa//ngang pa pad ma stan 'dug bri;;de'i
21b1(rgyud): nub tu bri ba ni//sa'i lha dang dbyangs can dang //khyab 'jug skye mched de bzhin klung //bde byed de bzhin bzla ba ste//nub kyi phyogasu gnas nas ni//rab tu nan tan byas te bri//bris nas sngags pas de
21b2(rgyud): nas ni;;yid la 'khrul pa med pa ru//gsang sngags mgon po'i 'og logasu//khro chen gzhan gyis mi thub ces//bya ba nyid dang lha mo ni//gzhan gyis mi thub ma dang ni//sa bdag lag na bum pa thogs;
21b3(rgyud): gus pas yang dag gnas//klu chen dga' bo nye dga' dag//shAkya seng ge skyob pa yi//sgo drung gnyisu mngon phyogs par//rab tu sgrims te bri bar bya//gzhan yang shAkya thub pa yi//rgyud rnaMs kun
21b4(rgyud): las dkyil 'khor du//sngags dang phyag rgya gang bshad pa//de dag kyang ni mkhas pas bri//sngags pa de nas phyir byung nas//cha gsum pa ni nges pa la//rdo rje dam pa sbyin pa ni//
21b5(rgyud): gur lta bu'i mdog can ste//spyi bor zur phud lnga dang ldan//byis pa gzhon nu'i gzugs can bri//ud pal sngon po'i rdo rje can//lag pa g.yon na thogs pa ste//pad ma dkar po'i stan la
21b6(rgyud): 'dug;;rab tu 'dzum pa'i bzhin du gnas//'od zer mtshan nyid bzang po yis//kun tu 'khor bar sngags pas bri//de'i byang du gzhon nu ni//dra ba'i 'od can lag pa na//rin chen dra ba thogs pa ste//
21b7(rgyud): sna tshogs pa'i rgyan gyis brgyan//pad ma dkar po'i stan( ) la 'dug//rgyal ba'i sras la lta ba bri//rdo rje dam pa sbyin pa yi//g.yon du pho nya mo lnga bri//skra can dang ni nyo skra can//sna tshogs ma dang ner can ma;
21b8(rgyud): de bzhin 'gug ma zhes bya ba//'di dag blo ldan pho nya mo//de dag gi ni 'od zer du//phyag rnyan ma ni lnga rnaMs dang //gzhon nu dra ba'i 'od can gyi//'khor gnyis po yang bri bar bya//de nas sngags pas lho phyog+sa
22a1(rgyud): @#//su;;byang chub seMs dpa' grags chen po//sgrib pa thaMs cad rnam sel gtso//yin bzhin nor bu thogs pa bri//de'i cha gnyis bor nas ni//dpa' bo ngo mtshar can dad ni//seMs can
22a2(rgyud): kun la mi 'jigs sbyin//ngan song thaMs cad spong ba dang//de bzhin skyong seMs blo gros dang//blo can snying rjes gdungs pa dang//byaMs pa mngon du 'byung ba dang//gdungs pa thaMs cad zhi byed pa//de bzhin blo
22a3(rgyud): gros bsam yas te//mkhas pas brgyad po bri bar bya//byang gi phyogasu song nas ni//sa'i snying po 'bar dang bcas//bzod dang des pa phun tshogs bri//de'i steng ni mkhas pa yis//'byung kung kun dang ldan
22a4(rgyud): pa yis;;rin chen kun gyis nyer brgyan pa//de'i steng na rin chen bzhi//pad ma yang dag gnas pa ni//yid du 'ong ba bsam byas te//byang chub seMs dpa' gragasachen po//de'i steng na 'dug pa bri//de'i
22a5(rgyud): 'khor ni yon tan gtso//mtha' yas shin du grangs med de//de'i steng nas byang chub seMs//rin chen 'byung gnas rin chen lag//sa 'dzin dang ni lag na ni//rin chen phyag rgya can
22a6(rgyud): dang ni;;lhag pa'i bsam pa brtan pa nyid//grags chen 'di dag bri bar bya//bdag nyid chen po de 'khor du//grags pa dag kyang bri bar bya//de 'khor dpa' bo chen po ni//namakha' dri m+de
22a7(rgyud): blo gros mkha'//blo gros rnam dag nyid dang ni//spyod pa yis ni blo gros dang//blo ni brtan pa zhes bya ste//de dag rab tu sgrib bri zhing//rtag tu dpa' bo 'di rnaMs dang//lhan cig go rims bzhin du bas
22a8(rgyud): de nas gsang ba po'i bdag po phyag na rdo rjes//thaMs cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor rnaMs las (de bzhin sngags pas nub phyogasu//dpa' bo namakha'i snying po ni//gzi brjid chen po gos dkar gyon//lag na ral gri thogs bsaMs te//rgyal sras pad ma'i bdan steng na//leg+sa par 'dug pa bri ba bya//
22b1(rgyud): ba 'di gling bzhi tshig tu smras pa//'jig rten du ni kun mkhyen rnaMs//u dum 'bar ba'i me tog bzhin//rgya lam na yang res 'ga' zhig//'byung 'gyur yang mi 'byung ngo//gsang sngags spyod pa'i
22b2(rgyud): tshul 'byung ba//de bas kyang ni dkon pa ste//skal pa bye ba du ma ru//sdig pa bgyis par gyur ma dag//de kun dkyil 'khor 'di 'dra bar//mthong bas byang bar 'gyur lags na//grags pa sngags spyad
22b3(rgyud): mtha' yas sngags spyad pa'i//tshul la gnas na smos ci 'tshal//skyob pa'i gsang sngags zlas nas ni//go 'phang bla na med par 'gyur//gang dag spyad mchog 'di la//blo gros shin du mi g.yo ba//de dag
22b4(rgyud): gi ngan 'gro'i rgyun//sdug bsngal kun 'byung rab tu spyad///de'i tshe thaMs cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de dag dang//rdo rje 'dzin pa de dag gis//mgrin gcig tu mthun pa'i tshig smras pa//gsang
22b5(rgyud): sngags spyad pa'i dbang gyur pa//dpa' bo chen po legaso leg+sa;khyod kyi yid la gang bsam pa//mnyam med thaMs cad mkhyen la zhus;sngags rgyud rtogs par shes pa yi//byang chub seMs dpa' gzhan rnaMs
22b6(rgyud): dang;;sngags spyad 'bras bu la gnas pa//tshon ni ji ltar bdag cag thaMs cad dbang du gyur//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la//phyag na rdo rje sa 'di skad ces gsol to//tshon zhes bgyi ba de gang lags//
22b7(rgyud): ci slad 'di ni tshon zhes bgyi//tshon ni ji ltar dge bar bgyi//gang gis thog mar btang bar bgyi//rta babs rnaMs de bzhin du//sgo khyud rnaMs kyi tshad ni//sgo khyud dang ni ci 'drar bgyi//mgon po yi ni bshad du
22b8(rgyud): gsol//lha bshos metog byug spos dang//bum ba rnaMs kyang rgyas par gsungs//slob ma ci ltar bzud pa dang//de ni ci ltar dbang skur dang//bla ma la ni yon 'bul dang//sbyin sreg gnas kyang bdag la
23a1(rgyud): @#//gsungs;;gsang sngags mtshan nyid ci 'dra lags//ting nge 'dzin du bzhed pa gang//de skad gsol nas bcom ldan 'das//chos kun dbang phyug pa yi//rdo rje 'dzin la 'di skad gsungs//
23a2(rgyud): yid gcig tu tshur nyon cig//theg chen 'bras bu 'byung ba yi//gsang sngags spyod pa tshul gyis mchog//seMs can chen po khyod kyis ni//gang dag dris pa bshad par bya//seMs can khams ni zhen byed cing//
23a3(rgyud): 'di ni chos dbyings ro yin par//sangs rgyas rnaMs kyi sngon gsungs te//de phyir de ni tshon zhes bya//tshon ni gang nas dge bar bya//phyi rol nas ni tshon min bgye//dkar po thog mar btang bar bya//de'i 'og
23a4(rgyud): tu dmar po ste//de nas ser po btang bar bya//de'i 'og tu ljang khu btang//kun gyi nang rol nag ste//tshon gyi cho ga de ltar bshad//rta babs kyi tshad kyang ni//dkyil 'khor dbu ma dang 
23a5(rgyud): mnyam mo;;sgo khyud dag kyang de dang mnyam//pad ma sor bcu drug tu bshad//dang po'i sgo ni snying pa yi//dkyil 'khor dang ni mnyam zhes bya//'jigs med dag ni rims kyis ni//
23a6(rgyud): bskyed mede las lhag zhes bya//sgo rnaMs kyi ni bar bar du//sgo srungs rnaMs ni bzhag par bya//ting nge 'dzin ni mdor na seMs//yang dag 'gu ba yin par bshad//seMs dpa' chen po khyad par ni;;yid
23a7(rgyud): gcig bsdus la mnyan par bgyis//sangs rgyas kun gyi ting nge 'dzin ni//stong pa nyid ces rgyal bar gsungs//de ni seMs yongs shes pa las//thob par 'gyur gyis gzhan du min//de dag nyid ni
23a8(rgyud): spangs pa ste//rdzogs sangs rgyas kyi yin par 'dod//stong pa chen po zhes bya ste//kun mkhyen rdzogs byed pa'o//de bas rnam pa thaMs cad du//stong nyid rtag tu dran par bya'o//de nas bcom
23b1(rgyud): ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad sangs rgyas thaMs cad dang thabs gcig yang dag par mos nas//nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas dang//byang chub seMs dpa' rnaMs kyi ting nge 'dzin so so'i tshul gsungs so//
23b2(rgyud): de'i tshe yang sangs rgyas thaMs dang rgyud gcig pa'i stobs kyi shugs zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugaso//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub seMs dpa' rnasaM kyi phyag na rdo rje la bka'
23b3(rgyud): stsal pa;byang chub snying por 'dug nas su//bdud ni rnam par bzhi bcom pas//de nas dpa' bo chen po zhes//tshangs pa laswogs pa lha tshogs la//'jigs pa thaMs cad med pa'i sgra//dga' bzhin
23b4(rgyud): du ni sgrogs par byed//de phyin cad ni nga dpa' bo//dpa' bo chen po zhes su grags//skye ba med par ngas rtogs pa//tshig gi spyod yul rnam spangs shing//nyes pa thaMs cad spangs pa ste//rgyu dang
23b5(rgyud): rkyen dang bral ba yin//stong pa'i ye shes mkhas 'dra ba//mun pa kun dang bral gyur cig//shin du dri med de kho na//ci ltar 'byung ba nga la 'byung//kun mkhyen tsam gyi tshul du gnas;;kun
23b6(rgyud): mkhyen sangs rgyas mtshan nyid do//nga'i de nyid dam pa 'di//'jig rten snang bar byed pa ste//byin gyi rlabs kyis stobs kyis ni//yig 'bru rnamasu bstan nas su//seMs can rnaMs la snying rje'i phyir//ngas
23b7(rgyud): ni thaMs cad rab tu bshad//de nas dpal ldan rdo rje 'dzin//ngo mtshar gyur nas mig bdangs te//thaMs cad mkhyen pa mgo bo yis//phyag 'tshal nas ni tshig 'di smras//sangs rgyas kun gyi thabs dang ni//
23b8(rgyud): shes rab bsam yas ngo mtshar che//spros pa kun dang bral ba yi//chos rtogs nas ni rang 'byung rnaMs//'jig rten kun gyi re ba kun//yongasu rdzogs par byed pa ston//gsang sngags mtshan nyid de lta bu//
24a1(rgyud): @#//bden pa gnyis la legs gnas pa//seMs can kun gyis shes nas ni//'jig rten kun gyi mchog gnas 'gyur//de nas gsang ba po'i bdag po phyag na rdo rjes tshigasu bcad pa de dag gsol n+sa;
24a2(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad la mig mi 'dzum pa'i lta ba ltas nas mi smra ba 'dug go//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis gsang ba'i bdag po phyag na rdo rje la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//gsang ba'i bdag
24a3(rgyud): po gzhan yang byang chub seMs dpa' skye gcig gis thogs pa//sangs rgyas kyi sa la gnas pa rnaMs kyi ting nge 'dzin ni/dgos pa dang ni bral bral ba ru//'jig rten mtshan nyid shes nas ni//dgos pa'i sa la
24a4(rgyud): gnas de rnaMs//sangs rgyas sa la gnas pa yin//gsang ba po'i bdag po gzhan yang //byang chub seMs dpa' sa brgyad pa'i dbang phyug rnaMs kyi tinge 'dzin ni/chos thaMs cad mi dmigs shing/srid dang skye
24a5(rgyud): ba rnaMs spangs pa yi//'jig rten sgyu ma ltar shes nas//'jig rten 'jig rten dbang du 'dod//gsang ba po'i bdag po gzhan yang nyan thos dmigs pa dang bcas pa'i sa la gnas pa rnams/
24a6(rgyud): skye ba dang 'jig par rtogs nas//mtha' gnyis bshig pas/rgyu mi dmigs pa'i phyir//shin du mi dngos po med pa'i ye shes mi spyod de/'di ni nyan thos kyi ting nge 'dzin gyi tshul lo//gsang ba po'i
24a7(rgyud): bdag po rang sangs rgyas dang 'bras bu rnam par bshig nas//brjod du med pa'i chos kyi tshul la gnas pa rnaMs ni/chos thaMs cad brjod du med de snyam pa'i ye shes 'jug ste//nga shin du 'gog pa'i ting nge 'dzin
24a8(rgyud): la yang dag par reg par byed pa 'di ni//rang sangs rgyas rnaMs kyi ting nge 'dzin gyi tshulalo//gsang ba'i bdag po gzhan yang 'jig rten pa rnaMs rgyu las 'bras bu dang las 'byung ba 'am/ldog par rtog cing
24b1(rgyud): gzhan gyi dbang gis stong pa nyid kyi ting nge 'dzin kun tu skye bar 'gyur te/'di ni 'jig rten pa rnaMs kyi ting nge 'dzin gyi tshul lo//gsang ba po'i bdag po 'di ni ting nge 'dzin gyi tshul yin te/de la gnas pa'i sangs 
24b2(rgyud): rgyas dang rang sangs rgyas dang//byang chub seMs dpa' skyob rnaMs dang//nyan thos de la sdig bcom par//grags pa gang dag yin pa dang//'jig rten pa'i lha gang yin pa dang//gsang sngags med pa'i cho ga'i tshul//
24b3(rgyud): seMs can rnaMs la phan 'dod pa//dpa' bo de tshe 'chad par byed//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis gsang ba po'i bdag po phyag na rdo rje la bka' stsal pa//gsang ba po'i bdag pa gsang sngags kyi mtshan nyid
24b4(rgyud): nyon cig/bcom ldan 'das de ltar 'tshal lo zhes gsol//bcom ldan 'das la nyan pa dang/bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di skad ces gsang ba'i bdag po de la/rdzogs sangs rgyas kyi gsang ba ni/
24b5(rgyud): tshig dang mig dang rnga yis mtshan//tshig de dbang du gyur pa na//gsang sngags don rnaMs thaMs cad sgrub//kha cig skul ba'i tshig dang ldan//kha cig rang ming thog mar smos//hUM dang phaT dang ldan pa ni//
24b6(rgyud): gtsug tor rnaMs kyi yin par sgrags//zung zo tshom cig ces pa dang//snun sod drol cing ces pa ni//phyag brnyan rnaMs dang khro bo yi//gsang sngags yin pas 'dren pas gsungs//na ma swA hA laswogs ldan/
24b7(rgyud): ting nge 'dzin gyi rtogs pas mtshan//zhi ba'i yige rnam par dag//re ba thaMs cad rdzogs byed pa'i//gsang sngags sangs rgyas thaMs cad dang//byang chub seMs dpa' skyob pa yin//nyan thos rnaMs kyi gsang
24b8(rgyud): sngags ni//gcig pu bzhi ni gcig la gnas//rang sangs rgyas kyi nga bzhin te//khyad par dag ni bshad bya na//ting nge 'dzin gyi khyad par las//las tshe rnam par sbyong zhes bya//gsang ba po'i bdag po gzhan
25a1(rgyud): @#//yang gsang sngags rnasaM kyi mtshan nyid ni sangs rgyas thaMs cad kyis ma byas/byed du ma bcug rjes su yid rang ba ma yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na//'di ni chos rnaMs kyi chos nyid de//'di ltar de
25a2(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa rnaMs byung yang rung ma byung yang rung//chos rnaMs kyi chos nyid de na ye nas gnas pa ste//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de yang 'di ltar gsang sngags kyi chos nyid do//gsang ba po'i bdag po de la gang gi tshe/
25a3(rgyud): yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas thaMs cad mkhyen pa/thaMs cad gzigs pa 'jig rten byung ba de'i tshe//chos nyid de tshul sna tshogs dang//dgongs pa sna tshogs kyis sesaM can bsam pa sna tshogs pa rnaMs la
25a4(rgyud): tshig sna tshogs pa rnaMs/yig 'bru sna tshogs dang/yul gyi nges pa'i tshig tu brjod pa sna tshogs dang/dbyangs sna tshogasu go ba rnaMs su byin gyis rlabs te/gsang sngags kyi tshul 'chad do//ci ltar de bzhin
25a5(rgyud): gshegs pa rnaMs gsang sngags yig 'bru dag tu byin gyis rlob ce na/gsang ba po'i bdag po//de la de bzhin gshegs pa rnaMs kyis skal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du mar yang dag par sgrubs
25a6(rgyud): pa'i bden pa'i tshig gang yin pa dang//'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi dang/dran pa nye bar bzhag pa bzhi dang/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi dang//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang/pha rol du
25a7(rgyud): phyin pa drug dang/byang chub kyi yan lag rin po che bdun dang/tshangs pa'i gnas bzhi dang/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad gang dag yin pa dang/gsang ba po'i bdag po //'dor na de bzhin gshegs
25a8(rgyud): pa rnaMs kyi thaMs cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes gang yin pa de ni thaMs cad /de bzhin gshegs pa rnaMsakyi bsod naMs dang//ye shes kyi stobs dang /sngon gyi smon lam mkhyen pa'i stobs dang/chos kyi dbyings kyi cho ga de
25b1(rgyud): yang gang zhe na/bzod nas ma skyes pa'i phyir/a ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'o//dgos pa dang bral ba'i phyir//ka ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'i//namakha' dang 'dra bar dmigasu med pa'i phyir//kha ni chos thaMs
25b2(rgyud): cad kyi sgo'o//'gro ba thaMs cad dmigasu med pa'i phyir/ga ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo//gcig tu 'phungs pa dmigasu med pa'i phyir/g+ha ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'//'chi 'pho thaMs cad dang bral ba'i phyir;;
25b3(rgyud): tsa ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo 'o//grib ma dang mtshungs pa'i phyir//tsha ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'o //skye ba dmigasu med pa'i phyir/dza ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'o
25b4(rgyud): cad kyi sgo'o//nga rgyal dmigasu med pa'i phyir/Ta ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgA+o'o//skye ba dmigasu med pa 'i phyir//Tha ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgoro//dri ma dmigasu med pa'i phyir//dz+ha ni chos thaMs
25b5(rgyud): chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'o//slu ba yongasu bral ba'i phyir//d+ha ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgro'o//de bzhin nyid mi dmigs pa'i phyir//ta ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'i//'khrug pa dmigasu med pa'i phyir;;Ta ni
25b6(rgyud): chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'o//'dul ba dmigasu med pa'i phyir //da ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'o/khams dmigasu med pa'i phyir//d+ha ni chos thaMd kyi sgo'o//don dam pa'i bden pa dmigasu med pa'i phyiro//
25b7(rgyud): pa ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo/'o//dbu dang 'dra bar snying po med pa'i phyir //pha ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo 'o//ngag gi lam dang bral ba'i phyir//ba ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'//dbu ba dang 'dra bar snying po medapI phyir;
25b8(rgyud): pha ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgau'o//pha ni cho srid pa thaMs cad dmigasu med pa'i phyir//b+ha ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgoro//'chi ba dmigasu med pa'i phyir/ma ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgor//theg pa thaMs cad dmigasu
26a1(rgyud): /med pa'i phyir//ya ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'o//rdul thaMs cad dang bral ba'i phyir//ra ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo 'o//mtshan nyid mi dmigs pa'i phyir//la ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'i;;rang bzhin
26a2(rgyud): gyis zhi ba'i phyir//sha ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'//rang bzhin gyis blun pa nyid kyi phyir//4 ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo//bden pa thaMs cad dmigasu med pa'i phyir//sa ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgA+o'o//rgyud dmigasu
26a3(rgyud): med pa'i phyir//ha ni chos thaMs cad kyi sgo'o //gsang ba bo'i bdag po//nga dang nya dang na dang ni ni ting nge 'dzin thaMs cad la dbang sgyur ba ste//mthu thaMs cad dang ldan pa//rab tu myur bar dgongs pa'i don
26a4(rgyud): rab tu bsgrub par byed pa ma yin no//gsang ba'i bdag po//gsang sngags kyi tshul ting nge 'dzin gyi sgo 'di ni/re ba thaMs cad yongasu rdzogs par byed pa//de bzhin gshegs pa thaMs cad kyi rnam par smin
26a5(rgyud): pa'i 'bras bu bsam gyis mi khyab pa//rnam pa thaMs cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa//gsang sngags kyi de kho na nyid kyi don ma nges pa//dus gsum las shin du 'das pa//namakha' ltar dri ma
26a6(rgyud): med pa ste//bsam yas seMs la gnas//las dang bya ba dag la byung//spyad pa'i sa ni rjes thob nas//bsam yas 'bras bu smin par byed//'di ni de nyid dam pa ste//sangs rgyas kun
26a7(rgyud): gyis rab tu bstan//gsang sngags cho ga 'di shes nas//sngags pa dngos grub thob par 'gyur//sgra zhes bya ba de nyid mchog//gsang sngags rnaMs kyi gsang sngags mtshan//sngags pa yid la seMs na ni;;'chi
26a8(rgyud): 'pho med pa'i gnas thob po//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la//gsang ba po'i bdag po phyag na rdo rjes//'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das kyis gsang sngags kyi mtshan nyid kyi tshul bsam gyis
26b1(rgyud): mi khyab pa'o//chos kyi rnam grangs bstan pa/bcom ldan 'das ngo mtshar lagaso//de'i nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas thaMs cad mthun mong ma lags te//bcom ldan 'das gang dag gis sngags
26b2(rgyud): kyi tshul 'di la dad pa'i seMs can de dag kyang thun mong ma yin pa'i yon tan dang ldan par 'gyuro//bcom ldan 'das gzhan yang dkyil 'khor gyi yo byad kyi cho ga bshad du gsol//de skad ces gsol pa dang
26b3(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyis gsang ba po'i bdag po phyag na rdo rje la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//de nas gsang ba de yis su//me tog ser po dkar po dmar//yid du 'ong bas nan tan du//lha rnams
26b4(rgyud): la ni mchod par bya//pad ma 'am yang na ud pa la//na ga ge sar pu ga na//tsam pa a sho ti la ka//ba ta la dang sa la 'ang rung//de la swogs pa'i me tog rnaMs//yid du 'ong
26b5(rgyud): zhing lta na sdug//bkra shis pa la sngags pa yis//mkhas pas nan tan mchod pa bya//tsan dan ka ra spri ka dang//gur gum dang ni ru rta yang rung//spos rab tu bzang po ni//sna tshogs yid
26b6(rgyud): du 'bul la//a ka ru 'am sgron shing ngam//ga bur dang ni tsan dan dang//sa la'i thang chu bkra shis pa 'am//shi ri ba sa ka yang rung//gzhan yang gdug spos sna tshogs pa//bkra shis 'jig rten
26b7(rgyud): rnam grags pa//yid 'ong sngags pas cho go bzhin//lha rnaMs la ni dbul bar bya//de nas gzhan yang seMs dpa' che//lha bshos la ni cho ga bzhin//'o thug 'bras chan zhor sbyar dang//la phug
26b8(rgyud): dang ni khur skya dang//gar bod ka ra yid 'ong dang//kha 'da'i bca' ba bzang po dang//pu ri ka dang g+ha ri ka//de bzhin ma thu sha ri dang//phe na a shog bar de dang//par pa ta yang dbul bar bya//
27a1(rgyud): //de laswogs ba'i za ba dang//shing thog rnaMs ni sna tshogs dang//hags dang li ga ra rnaMs dang//bu ram sbrang rtsi mar dag dang//btung ba rnam pa sna tshogs dang//zho dang 'o ma rgyas par 'bul/
27a2(rgyud): mar me rnam sna tshogs dang//gtsang zhing shin du bzang po'o//'bru mar rab dri zhim pas//rab tu kang ste dbul bar bya//phyogasu gdugs dang ba dan rnaMs//kha dog sna tshogs rab tu sgrengs//rta babs
27a3(rgyud): bzang po rnaMs kyang ni//dril bu dang bcas rab tu 'bul//yang na yid kyi mchod pa yi//thaMs cad nan tan bskyed de bya//de nas bum pa brgyad dam ni//bcu drug rgyas par blangs nas ni//sman dang rin
27a4(rgyud): chen thaMs cad blugs//spos kyi chu ni rab tu bkang//ldum bu dag gis kha rgyan te//de bzhin 'bras bu kha yang gdangs//me tog spos laswogs pas brgyan//shin du bzang po srung bar bya//'grin pa ras
27a5(rgyud): kyis bcings pa rnaMs//mkhas pas rnam par bkod par bya//lha'i gtso bo rnaMs la ni//ras ni zung re zung re dbul//seMs dpa' chen po phal rnaMs la//ya re dbul ba rab tu sgrags//
27a6(rgyud): mchod pa de ltar byas na ni//slob ma dkyil 'khor drung bkug ste//sngags pas chu yis gsang tor bya//dri dang me tog sbyin par bya//de bzhin gshegs pa dran byas nas//byang chub
27a7(rgyud): seMs kyang 'dzin du bzhug//de nas rgyal ba'i rigs dge la//thaMs cad rab tu skyes par 'gyur//chos kyi dbyings las byung ba yi//phyag rgya de dag rnaMs la cing//chos kyi 'khor lo bcings nas ni//
27a8(rgyud): rdo rje seMs dpa' byin gyis brlab//de nas sangs rgyas kun gyis ni//dam tshig sdom brtson can gyis bcing//de'i sngags kyi cho ga bzhin//ras la lan gsum gtab par bya//snying rjer gyur pa'i yid
27b1(rgyud): kyis su//de dag gi ni dong yang gab//dam tshig lan gsum zlas na ni//spyi bor yi ge ra zhe pa//glad kyi 'khor bcas brjid dang ldan//'bar ba'i phreng ba rnam par 'phro//zla ba char ga lta bu yi//
27b2(rgyud): kha dog dkar po bzhag par bya//skyob pa kun gyi spyan sngar ni//de nas me tog 'dor du bzhug//me tog gang la babs pa de//sngags pa'i de la sbyin par bya//dkyil 'khor dang po'i sgo yi drung //sgo
27b3(rgyud): khyud kyi ni dbus kyi cha//dkyil 'khor gyi ni sgo gnyis kyi//bar du slob ma bzung bar bya//der gnas pa la cho ga kun//rim pa bzhin du legs par bya//slob ma sdig dang bral ba kun//de ltar leg+sa
27b4(rgyud): par btsun nas ni//de dag zhi bar bya ba'i phyir//de dag sbyin sreg cho ga bzhin du bya//snying po'i dkyil 'khor kyi ni 'dun//dkyil 'khor gnyis pa rgyab rol gyi//dkyil 'khor gyi ni
27b5(rgyud): dbus kyi char//ma yengs pa yi sesaM kyis bya//me thab khu gang tsam gyis ni//tshad du rab tu sgrags pa yin//kha ran sor bzhi tshad du bya//dbus su rdo rje rab tu rtag//de nas sngags
27b6(rgyud): pa'i g.yas logasu//sbyin sreg gi ni yo byad bzhag//g.yon gyi phyogasu slob ma rnaMs//tsog tsog por ni 'khor du bzhug//sdom brtson bdag nyid sa steng du//ku sha ting pa'i stan la 'dug//yang na
27b7(rgyud): tshon rtsi dmar po mchog//rab tu bzang po blangs nas ni//de yis me'i gnas rnaMs kun//sngags pa'i mdo ru bri bar bya//ku sha dag gi g.yas phyogs nas//thasaM cad 'khor yug dgram pa ni//mang
27b8(rgyud): por bskor ba'i tshul du bkram//spos kyi chu yis gsang tor bya//de nas sesaM can phan byed pa'i//me ni thab khung na bsaMs te//rtul shugs bzang pos gang blugs mchog//gsang sngags 'di yis
28a1(rgyud): @#//sbyin par bya//na ma sa man ta bud d+ha nan/ag ne ye swA hA//de nas slob ma re re nas//mkhas pa'i lag pa g.yon pa yis//g.yas pa'i mthe bo bzung nas su//mnyam par
28a2(rgyud): bzhag pa'i sbyin sreg bya//yid ni mnyam par bzhag nas su//sreg lugs re re las kyang ni//gsang sngags cho ga bzhin zlos shing//nyi shu rtsa gcig sbyin sreg bya//na ma sa man ta bung d+ha nan;a
28a3(rgyud): ma ha shwan ti ka ra pra sha ma d+ha ra ba ma nir dzA ta/a b+ha ba d+ha rma sa ma ta pra b+ha ba ta swA hA////sbyin sreg rjes la mkhas pa yi//slob ma rnaMs la yon slang ba//gser dang dngul dang rin chen dang;;rta dang
28a4(rgyud): de bzhin glang po dang//rnga mo ba glang ma he gos//gzhan yang dngos pa ci yang rung//de tshe slob mas gus par ni//dad pa rab tu ldan pa yis//seMs la dga' ba bskyed nas su//bla ma la ni yul
28a5(rgyud): 'bul lo//yang na ci nas bla ma de//rab tu 'gu bar 'gyur bar bya//de ltar byas nas bdag srung ste//slob ma de bor sgom pa ni//'di ni bsod naMs zhing yin zhes;;seMs
28a6(rgyud): can kun gyi don gyi phyir//skyob pa rnaMs kyi kun gyi gsung+sa;;rnam dag yon tan rgyas pa yi//dge mdun la ni kun gyis byin//dge mdun la phul 'bras bu che//skyes bu mkhas pa gang dag
28a7(rgyud): gis;;dge 'dun la ni yon 'bul na//longs spyod chen po mi bzad pa//'jig rten 'thad pa skye bar 'gyur//de bas thaMs cad mthun par ni//seMs la dga' ba bskyes nas su//sangs
28a8(rgyud): rgyas sngags pa'i dge 'dun la//ci gus par ni mchod bstod bya//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis rnam par snang mdzad//byang chub sems dpa' seMs dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rje la bka' stsal pa;
28b1(rgyud): sesaM dpa' chen po dbang skur ba//sngon gyi sangs rgyas nyid kyis bshad//rgyas pa ru ni bshad par bya//yid cig bsdus te legs par nyon//gsang ba po'i bdag po//de nas slob dpon gyi rtsa
28b2(rgyud): ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi mdun du bar khru do bor nas/phyi rol gyi sa dkyil 'khor gnyis pa gru bzhi pa/sgo gcig pa bri'o//gro bzhir rdo rje 'dzin pa bzhi bkod//bzhi gang zhe na/'di lta ste//spros pa med
28b3(rgyud): par gnas pa//namakha' dri ma med pa dang//dri ma med pa yi mig dang//gos sna tshogs gon pa rnamaso//dbus su pad ma chen po'i 'dab ma brgyad pa ze ba dang bcas pa bzhag ste/'dab ma bzhi la/
28b4(rgyud): byang chub sesaM dpa' grogasu gyur pa/sngon gyi smon lam gyi mthu can bzhi bri bar bya ste/'di lta ste/gzungs kyi dbang phyug rgyal po dang//dran pa dang shes bzhin can dang//phan pa'i lhag pI
