62b7(rgyud): mi zad pa bkod pa bstan pa dang/gsang sngags kyi spyad pa mngon par bstan pa 'di nyid kyis seMs can khams thaMs cad la ma lus par bdon pa thugs dam bcas pa yongasu rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir/chos kyi
62b8(rgyud): dbyings kyi mdzod kyi mthu rlabs la/de bzhin gshegs pa nyi ma pa nyid bkod pa'i snying po can zhes bya ba'i ting 'dzin la snyoMs par bzhugaso//bcom ldan 'das snyoMs par bzhugs ma thag tu/de nas de nyid
63a1(rgyud): /kyi tshe sems can gyi khams ma lus pa nges par 'byung bar byed pa'i sgo ba spu'i bu ga thaMs cad nas/bsam pa ji ltar bzhin las dang/tshe dang rnam par smin pa'i 'bras bu za ba dang/zar 'jug
63a2(rgyud): pa'i gzugs dang kha dog dang/dbyibs dang nga ro dang /'gro ba dang spyod pa dang/nges pa'i tshig brjod pa/sems can thaMs cad so so yang dag par mgu byed pa'i chos kyi dbyings rnam par skyed pa'i sku rnaMs 'byung
63a3(rgyud): bar 'gyur ro//byung nas kyang/namakha' dang mnyam pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams rab 'jam rnamasu/sgra dbyangs gcig gis chos kyi dbyings khyad par rnam par rig par byed pa/de bzhin gshegs 'byung bar
63a4(rgyud): 'gyur ba'i tshigasu bcad pa 'di sgrogs par gyur to//chos kyi mtshan nyid gang las ni//chos rnaMs dag ni thams cad dang//sangs rgyas rnaMs dang nyan thos dang//rang rgyal skyob pa rnaMs dang ni/byang
63a5(rgyud): chub seMs dpa' bo dang//de bzhin rgyal ba mchog rnaMs dang//seMs can sngon 'jig rten dag/btags pa rim pa bzhin 'byung zhing//chos rnaMs skyed dang gnas pa dag//gang las rtag tu
63a6(rgyud): 'byung 'gyur ba//shes rab thabs dang ldan 'gyur zhing//yid gnyis som nyi dang bral ba//rdzogs sangs rgyas kyis rab bshad pa'i//lam 'dir khyod kyis sgom par gyis//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku chos
63a7(rgyud): kyi dbyings las byung ba bdag gis chos kyi dbyings thaMs cad rang gi sku rnam par rig par byed pa /sprul pa'i sprin gyis yongasu bkang nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyis dbang tsam gyis ba spu'i bu ga
63a8(rgyud): thaMs cad nas/sangs rgyas 'byung ba cig nas gcig rgyud pa byin gyis rlabs te /rang rang gi chos kyi dbyings kyi gnasu bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub seMs dpa' phyag na rdo rje la
63b1(rgyud): bka' stsal pa/gsang ba po'i bdag po/gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor rnam par bkod pa/lha rnaMs kyi gnas yang dag par gnas par bsam pa sa bon dang bcas pas mtshan pa yod kyis/de nyon la legs par
63b2(rgyud): yid la zung shig dang/ngas khyod la bshad do//de skad bka' stsal pa dang/gsang ba po'i bdag po phyag na rdo rje bcom ldan 'das la nyan no//bcom ldan 'das kyis de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal pa/dang por sngags
63b3(rgyud): pas dkyil 'khor de//rang gi lus la bgod bya ste//rkang pa nas ni chu so la//thug gi bar du sa yi bsam//de nas gong du snying kha'i bar//sngon rtson can gyi chu yi bsam//chu yi yan chad me'i ste//me'i yan chad rlung
63b4(rgyud): yin no;;bsaMs nas de dag mkhas pa yis//sa la dag gzugs rnyan bzhag par bya//de nas phyag na rdo rje bcom ldan dangs rnam par snang mdzad kyi/sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi sa la bzhug+sa nas
63b5(rgyud): chos mnyam pa nyid la ltas te/ma 'ongs pa'i skye bo rnaMs la rtags na/rigs sngags kyi rgyal mo the tsom thaMs cad gcod par byed pa 'di smraso//na ma sa man ta bu ta nan/a sa ma ba ta d+harma d+ha tu kA r+ye ti
63b6(rgyud): ga ta nan/sarba tha a kha a a sang sa hang ha/rang ra bang ba swAhA/hUM mahA swAhA/rang ra swAhA//gsang ba po'i bdag po phyag na rdo rje gsang sngags kyi rgyal po chen po 'dis smras ma thag tu/de nas phyogs bcu'i 'jig
63b7(rgyud): rten gyi khams na bzhugs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa thaMs cad kyi phyag g.yas pa rkyang ste/phyag na rdo rje'i spyi bor bzhag nas legaso zhes bya ba byin te/'di skad ces gsungaso//rigs kyi bu gang khyod kyis;bcoM
63b8(rgyud): ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyis la bzhugs nas/thaMs cad du gsang sngags kyi tshul la mnyam pa nyid du gnas pa'i byang chub seMs dpa' rnams la snang bar bya bar 'dod pa'i
64a1(rgyud): @#//phyir;gsang sngags kyi rgyal mo 'di smras pa legaso legaso//de ci'i phyir zhe na/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas
64a2(rgyud): byang chub kyi snying po la bzhugs te/tshig bcu gnyis po 'di dag gis chos kyi dbyings rnam par spyad cing bdud bzhi phan par byas nas/chos kyi dbyings nas byung ba 'di gnas gsum nas 'byung ste/bdud sdig
64a3(rgyud): can rnaMs btul bar 'gyur ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs mnyam pa nyid rnyes nas/namakha' tshad med pa'i phyir/sku tshad med pa rnyes par 'gyur//de bzhin du gsung dang thugs tshad
64a4(rgyud): med pa yang brnyes par 'gyur to//de ltar ye shes tshad med pa dang ldan zhing /chos thaMs cad la dbang thob pas/'di ltar gsang sngags kyi rgyal po tshig bcu gnyis po 'di da gnyid las rtsaMs te
64a5(rgyud): chos 'chad do//rigs kyi bu 'on kyang bcom ldan 'das rnam par nang mdzad kyi sku dang/gsung dang thugs mnyam pa nid la zhugs pas na/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas bzhin du snang ngo//thaMs cad
64a6(rgyud): mkhyen pa rdzogs sangs rgyas//bcom ldan rnam par snang mdzad la//sngags spyad tshig mchog zhus shig dang//cho ga rgyas pa bka' stsal to//nges kyang sngon gyi 'di rnaMs kyis//bla med byang chub rtogs nas su
64a7(rgyud): mya ngan 'das lam 'gro ba yi//mnyam pa med pa'i chos bshad pa//'jig rten khams na bzhugs pa yi//rdzogs sangs rgyas rnaMs dbang du gyur//de nas dpal ldan rdo rje 'dzin//bsam pa mchog rab
64a8(rgyud): mgu 'gyur pa//rdzogs sangs rgyas kyis byin rlabs pas//'di skad ces ni tshig smras pa//chos 'di rnaMs ni mtshan nyid med//ngo bo nyid med gnas med pa//las dang tshe las rnam par grol;rdzogs
64b1(rgyud): sangs rgyas 'dra bar 'dod//thugs rje mnga' mdzad pa'i//mgon po rnaMs kyi thabs kyis ni//rnam par skul nas chos rnaMs kyi//mtshan nyid 'di dag rtogs par 'gyur//de nas gsang ba po'i bdag po phyag na rdo rjes
64b2(rgyud): ched du brjod pa'i tshigasu bcad pa de smras nas/ma 'ongs pa'i 'gro ba rnaMs la ltas te snying rje chen po'i snying po//'byung ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po 'di'i the tsom bcad pa'i phyir/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad la
64b3(rgyud): zhus pa;;seMs can kun gyi don gyi phyir//kun mkhyen snyung dang bral ba'o//the tsom thaMs cad gcod mdzad pa//'dren pa khyod la zhu ba ni//dkyil 'khor gyi ni sngon rol du//ci zhig bgyi ba 'dren pas gsungs;;thub
64b4(rgyud): pa chen po slob dpon dang//slob ma'ang rnam pa du ru bshad//sa gzhi ci 'drar 'tshal bar bgyi//de yang ci ltar sbyar bgyid//ci ltar sa ni dag par 'gyur//ci ltar de ni rtan par lags//slob ma'i
64b5(rgyud): dgos pa gang gis gyi//sbyang ba 'am 'dren pas bka' stsol cig//dag pa'i mtshan ma ci 'dra dang//gang gis de dag srung bar bgyi//sa ni gang gis byin gyis rlabs//der dang po gang gis rtag;
64b6(rgyud): pa'i che ba thig gdab pa'i//srad bu rnam par du 'tshal bgyi//mchod pa rnam pa dur 'tshal bgyi//me tog gdug spos ci 'dra dang//me tog dang ni bdug spos dag//ci ltar dbul ba 'dren pas gsungs//lha bshos
64b7(rgyud): sbyin sreg las rnaMs kyang//gang dag la ni ci ltar bgyi//lha rnaMs kyi ni bdan dag kyang//gang la gang gis bgyi ba gsungs//kha dog dbyibs dang gzugs rnaMs kyang//go rims bzhin du mgon pos gsungs;;lha rnaMs
64b8(rgyud): dang ni phyag rgya dang//bdan gyi tshad kyang bshad du gsol//phyag rgya rnaMs ni gang nas byung//ci'i phyir phyag rgya zhes kyang bgyi//dbang skur rnam pa du 'tshal bgyi//dam tshig rnam pa du zhig mchis//sngags spyad pa la
65a1(rgyud): /brtson pa yi//sngags pa byang chub seMs dpa' dang//thaMs cad mkhyen pa ci ltar 'gyur//ci ltar bden pa mthong bar 'gyur//dngos grub rnam pa dur 'tshal bgyi//grub nas dus kyang bka' stsol
65a2(rgyud): cig;;grub nas nam mkha' 'gro ba dang//ci ltar bden pa mthongabar 'gyur//dngos gtso bo lus 'di ma thob par//ci ltar lha'i lus thob 'gyur//sprul pa rnaMs ni sna tshogs pa//de la gang nas 'byung bar 'gyur//
65a3(rgyud): nyi zla me dang phyogs rnaMs dang//rgyu skar bza' dang skar ma dang//yud tsam dang ni thang cig dang//dga' dang 'chi dang rgud pa dang//ltas ngan dag ni thaMs cad dang//de la ci ltar 'byung mi 'gyur//rkang gnyis rtso bo sangs
65a4(rgyud): rgyas dang;;sngags pa ci ltar 'bral mi 'gyur//'jig rten sbyin sreg las dang med dag dang//tshim par bgyi ba dur 'tshal gyi//lha rnaMs dang ni so so yi//ngo bo nyid ni 'dren pas gsungs;;'jig
65a5(rgyud): rten pa'i sngags rnaMs dang//de bzhin 'jig rten 'das pa yi//tshad dang 'bras bu grangs rnaMs dang//ting nge 'dzin gyi khyad par dang//las kyi rnam pa gang du smin//ci ltar rnam par smin mi 'gyur//
65a6(rgyud): sngags pa rnaMs ni nam zhig naM//las dang tshe las rnam 'grol 'gyur//de nas kun mkhyen rdzogs sangs rgyas//bcom ldan snyung dang bral ba yi//rdo rje 'dzin la 'di skad gsungs//snying rje chen po'i rtsa las byung;
65a7(rgyud): gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor rnam dgod pa//lha rnaMs kyi ni gnas nges pa//pa chen po'i go 'phang mchog//rdzogs sangs rgyas kyi rab gsang ba//dpa' bo cher 'byung khyod kyis gsang//yongasu dris pa de legs so//
65a8(rgyud): lag na rdo rje stobs chen la//mdor ni sdus nas bshad par bya//dkyil 'khor ni sngon rol du//bya ba rnaMs ni nges par nyon//so so'i las rnaMs 'dod pa yis//tshul shes pas ni ci rigs par//rang gi ting 'dzin la
65b1(rgyud): gnas te;;rigs sngags rgyal po stobs po che//yan lag bcu gnyis tshig 'byung ba//go rims bzhin du zlas rjod bya//gsang sngags phyag rgya rtogs shes pa'i//slob dpon rnam pa gnyis shes bya//zab dang
65b2(rgyud): mi zab bye brag las//de'i mtshan nyid shes par bya//zab mo rgya cher shes par bya//lus can rnaMs ni yid mi rton//sangs rgyas rnaMs kyi sras mchog ste//mthong ba'i chos ni rnam par spangs//gnyis pa mthong ba'i
65b3(rgyud): chos rnaMs kyis//myos par gyur pa dmigs pa can//sangs rgyas rkang gnyis mchog rnaMs kyis//'jig rten pa'i dkyil 'khor kun//de yis bris par bya ba'i phyir//rjes ma nang zhing dbang yar skur;;dus dang
65b4(rgyud): dus min bye brag las//slob ma de'i rgyal ba'i mchog//sa ni dang por shes par bya//sems kyi sar ni bshad pa yin//sbyang sa sngar ni stan ba yin//de yis de ni yongasu sbyang//nam zhig nyes dang
65b5(rgyud): bral ba na//seMs kyi sa ni mi 'jigs pa//de tshe de ni dag pa ste//nyes pa thaMs cad rnam par spangs//mngon par byang chub mthong bas na//de ltar de ni brtan shes bya//de las gzhan du sa
65b6(rgyud): spong ba;;de ni dag par mi 'gyuro//sags pa rtog la gnas nas su//gang zhig sa sbyong byed pa ni//byang chub seMs ni rnam spangs pa//gsang ba'i bdag po de mi 'dra//de bas rtog pa rnam spangs nas;;sa kun
65b7(rgyud): rnam par sbyang bar bya//nga yis dkyil 'khor rnaMs kyi ni//cho ga rgyas par gang bstan pa//de dag thog mar bstan pa ni//rmongs pa rnaMs kyis mi shes te//rtog pa sdug bsngal rgyur 'gyur ba//ci srid ma spangs b+Ra
65b8(rgyud): du ni;;'jig rten 'di ni sangs rgyas min//kun mkhyen zhes kyang mi bya'o//slob ma rnaMs la nan tan tu//byang chub seMs kyis sbyang bar bya//srung ba mi g.yo mgon po 'am//khams gsum rnam rgyal bas bya//
66a1(rgyud): @#//slob ma nam zhig dmigs pa yis//nam yang g.yos par mi 'gyur ba//de tshe dri med namakha' bzhin//yang dag par ni dag par 'gyur//sa byin rlabs sa dang po ni//rdzogs sangs rgyas
66a2(rgyud): kyis bya bar bshad//gnyis pa seMs kyi dbang gyis te//'dir ni bzhan du mi bya'o//srad bu rnam pa bzhir bshad de//dkar po ser po dmar po dang//nag po zhes ni bya bar rjod//lnga pa namakha' lta bur bshad//
66a3(rgyud): bar snang la ni bzung nas su//der ni dkyil 'khor rtag par bya//srad bu dag ni gnyis pa yis//sa la dkyil 'khor dgod par bya//sangs rgyas rnams ni thaMs cad dang//srad bu slo can rnaMs kyi bdan;;pad ma
66a4(rgyud): yid las byung ba'i mchog//bkra shis 'jig rten rnaMs kyi sngags//rang rgyal dang ni dgra bcom pa//phyogs gcig shes par gang grags pa//de dag bstan du shes par bya//pad ma las byung ba'i
66a5(rgyud): 'dab ma'o//'jig rten gyi ni lha rnaMs gang//tshangs laswogs pa 'jig rten grags//de dag stan ni pad ma dmar//rgyal po rnaMs kyis sngags pa'o//phal pa rnaMs kyis ci rigs par;sa'i
66a6(rgyud): bye brag gang las bsam//mchod pa rnam bzhir shes bya ste//phyag 'tshal thal mo sbyar ba dang//de bzhin byasaM dang snying rje dang//metog gdug spos 'jig rten pa'o//sor mo las byung metog rnaMs//skyob pa
66a7(rgyud): kun la dbul bar bya//yan lag kun 'byung phyag rgya ni//bcing nas byang chub seMs sgoms te//so sor mgon po thaMs cad dang//de sras rnaMs la nan tan bya'o//'di ni skyon med metog ste//rab tu gsung zhing
66a8(rgyud): rnam par mdzes//chos kyi dbyings kyi ljon shing rnaMs//rgyal ba'i mchod pa'i phyir byung ba//ting nge 'dzin dbang gis sgyur ba yi//sngags kyis de dag byin rlabs na//chos kyi dbyings las byung ba yi;;sprin mang
66b1(rgyud): rgya chen dam pa rnaMs//sangs rgyas rnaMs kyi spyan sngar ni//de tshe rtag tu char bzhin 'bab//lha rnaMs phal pa gzhan la ni//metog 'di rnaMs sgrags pa ste//rang gi sngags rgyud rgyas shes pas//ci ltar rig par dbul
66b2(rgyud): bar bya//de bzhin metog bdug spos dri rnaMs//ci ltar rigs pas rnam par bsam//the bas srin lag sbyar nas ni//bkra shis pa zhes bshad pa ste//de yis metog de blangs nas//rang gi snying ka'i phyogs
66b3(rgyud): nas dbul;;'jig rten pa'i lha rnaMs la//lte ba'i thad ka nas shes bya//gdug spos khu tshur ram//yang na pad ma'i 'phreng ba yis//bar snang la ni bzung nas su//'jig rten mgon skyob rnams la dbul;
66b4(rgyud): de las gzhan pa 'jig rten pa'i//lha rnaMs la ni go rims bzhin//phyi dang nang gi bye brag gis//sbyin sreg rnam pa gnyis shes bya//las dang tshe las rnam par grol//sar yang sa bon 'byung (ba)ste/
66b5(rgyud): las 'dod pa'i sbyin sreg bya//nang gi sbyin sreg zhes su bshad//phyi rol gyi ni gsum ldan zhing//gnas gsum dang ni gsum la gnas//las gsum gyi ni lam grub pa//'jig rten pa' ni 
66b6(rgyud): sbyin pa mchog//sbyin sreg las ni mi shes pas//gang dag gzhan du byed pa ni//sngags kyi shes pa spangs pa ste//glen des 'bras mi thob po//de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang sngags dang//de bzhin rdzogs
66b7(rgyud): sangs rgyas kyis gsungs//kar dang ser du shes par bya//phyag rgya rdo rje thaMs cad dang//'jig rten dbang phyug gsang sngags ni//las kyi bye brag dag las dkar//kun tu gru bzhi lham pa dang//go rims bzhin
66b8(rgyud): du thaMs cad kyi//kha dog dbyibs dang gzugs rnaMs ni//bsam yas ye shes las byung bas//de bas de rnaMs bsam yas te//ye shes mkhyen gyi ye shes ni//seMs can don phyir tha dad gyur;;yid kyi rgya ni
67a1(rgyud): //ci tsam pa//bstan dang phyag rgya dag dang ni//lha'i tshad kyang go rims bzhin//de ci tsam du ni shes par bya//sangs rgyas gang nas byung ba nas//phyag rgya rnaMs kyang de nas byung//chos la byung
67a2(rgyud): ba'i slob ma rnaMs//chos kyi phyag rgya gdab par bya//de bas mdor na chos kyi dbyings//mtshan ma phyag rgya yin par bshad//dbang skur gsum du shes bya ste//de bas nan tan mnyam par bgyi//phyag rgya gang
67a3(rgyud): zhig dbang skur ba//las dang bya ba spangs pa ste//skyo ba rnaMs kyi dbang skur la//de ni mchog ste dang po yin//gnyis pa las dang bya ba yin//kun nas slang bar yang dag bshad//gsum pa yid kyi shes bya ste/
67a4(rgyud): yul dang dus ni spangs pa'o//bla ma legs par mgu byas nas//phan par byed pa gang smras pa'i//dbang skur 'di ni mchog yin par//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnaMs kyis sgrags//sangs rgyas rnaMs
67a5(rgyud): kyis mdor na ni//dam tshig can rnaMs sngar bshad de//dkyil 'khor 'di ni mthong ba las//dam tshig shes pa gcig ces bya//de phyag rgya mi bstan cing//sngags kyang sbyin par mi bya'o//byang chub
67a6(rgyud): seMs kyi bye brag las//sngags dang phyag rgya yongasu bzung//dkyil 'khor mthong zhes bya ba 'di//dam tshig can nyid shes bya ste//dkyil 'khor dang ni phyag rgya mthong//las dang bya ba la yang sbyar//bla mas
67a7(rgyud): lung ni ma byin par//de yang dam tshig shes par bshad//phyag rgya dkyil 'khor bri ba rnaMs//de tshe cho ga bzhin du bstan//gsang ba'i ye shes 'byung ba yi//dbang skur med pa'i sngags pa'ang yin//gsang ba'i
67a8(rgyud): dkyil 'khor dbus su ni//sngags pa cho gas dbang skur ba//dam tshig shes pa lngar shes bya//dam tshig can zhes gzhan mi bya//rtul shugs bzang po byang chub phyir//gang zhig seMs ni sgoms pa ni//sngags
67b1(rgyud): pa gnas ga mi dmigs pa//'di ni byang chub seMs dpa' yin//thabs kyi snying stobs bskyed nas su//seMs can don la sbyor ba na//dmigasu med pa'i spyod thob pa//rgyal ba'i mchog tu shes par bya;;thog ma
67b2(rgyud): nas ni zhi ba'i chos//ngo bo nyid ni med pa la//gang gis seMs ni rab zhi na//gnas pa de ni bden pa mthong//'di bden 'di ni yang dag pa//de bzhin du ni mi slu ba//ci ltar sangs rgyas bden gzigs pa//
67b3(rgyud): de bzhin sngon ni ngas mthong ba'i//byang chub seMs thob gang yin de//dngos grub de las gzhan ci dgos//de las gzhan du dngos grub ni//rnam pa dag ni sngar 'dod de//spyod pa'i rjesu 'jug pa dang;;de bzhin
67b4(rgyud): sa ni gnon pa dang//'jig rten mngon shes lnga 'dod dang;sangs rgyas nyid dang gang gzhan no//rgyud las rgyud du gyur pa yi//la ni ci srid ma nyaMs shing//rnam smon rnam par ma smin te//dngos grub
67b5(rgyud): dag tu dus su 'dod//mdor na gang tshe dus gcig tu//las dang skye ba 'phangs gyur pa//de tshe sngags pas dngos grub ni//yid las yang byung ba thob//ci ltar sgyu ma 'jigs med bzhin;
67b6(rgyud): sngags kyi dra bas rmongs byed pa//ji ltar mi 'phrul chen po bzhin//gang la las dang ngag med pa//dri za'i grong khyer ci bzhin te//de bzhin mi snang 'gyur zhes bya//de ni de na gnas pa yin//ji ltar nyal
67b7(rgyud): ba'i rmi lam na//sum bcu rtsa gsum gnasu phyin//lus kyang spangs pa ma yin la//de nyid kyis kyang de ma song//de bzhin rnal 'byor nyid kyis kyang//sngags pa sngags la gnas pa dag//bsod naMs bya ba'i
67b8(rgyud): las rnaMs kyis//'ja' dang 'dra ba'i lus kyang thob//lus dang ngag yid las byung pa'i//gsang sngags yid bzhin du 'dra ba//de las rnam par bsam pa'i don//ma rtags par yang 'byung bar 'gyur;;
68a1(rgyud): /ci ltar namakha' phyogs rnamasu//'dus byas spyod yul thaMs cad kyis//mi gos de bzhin gshegs pa yang//rtogs pa'i spyod yul kun gyis so//'di kun 'du shes tsam du ni|nam zhig
68a2(rgyud): de ltar sgoMs gyur cing//rnam par sgoms na sngags pa de//rdzogs sangs rgyas dang lhan cig dga'//sangs rgyas rkang gnyis gtso bo yis//sbyin sreg gnyisu bshad pa ni//phyi rol gyi dang nang gi steng/de yang
68a3(rgyud): tshim par bya dang bcas//lha rnaMs kyang ni so so yi//rang bzhin yongasu mkhyen nas su//'jig rten pa'i sngags rnaMs kyi//tshad dang legs par gsungs pa ni//dbang phyug la swogs mthu chen rnaMs//bkur ba'i lha
68a4(rgyud): rnaMs gang yin pa//de dag gis ni gang byas pa'i//sngags dang phyag rgya stobs chen rnaMs//'bras bu phal cher 'phral du ster//tshad ni bshad pa de yin no//'on kyang skye dang 'jig pa yi//chos can de
68a5(rgyud): dag rtan pa med//'jig rten 'das pa'i sngags rnaMs ni//thog ma'i byed pa med pa'o//las dang skye ba kun bcad pa//nyes pa gsum bral rgyal ba dang//rang rgyal gseru lta bu dang|de bzhin

94a7(rgyud): gang dran pa//de dang de la gtor ma sbyin//phyogs kyi gtor ma'i sngags ni/dbang ldan gyi phyogasu/ru tra ye swAhA/dbang po'i phyogasu/sha ga tra ya swAhA/me'i phyogasu/ag ne ye swAhA//gshin rje'i phyogasu༐
94a8(rgyud): bA+ai ba swAhA ya swAhA/bden bral gyi phyogasu/rAk+Sha pa d+hi pa ta ye swAhA/chu lha'i phyogasu/mA+e g+hA sha ni ye swAhA/rlung gi phyogasu/ba ya be swAhA/ku pe rA'i phyogasu/yak+Sha bi h+ya d+ha ri swAhA/krang kye re swAhA/srin po rnaMs kyi'o༐
94b1(rgyud): mi tsi mi tsi swAhA/sha za rnaMs kyi'o//gung i gung i/'byung po rnaMs kyi'o//de ltar kun tu gtor ma gtor la lag pa bkrus la nye reg byas la/de nas nang du zhugs te/sangs rgyas thaMs cad la phyag 'tshal nas
94b2(rgyud): sreg par bya ba'i rdzas gang cung zad yod pa de dag thaMs cad/'phags pa mi g.yo bas gsang te g.yas rol du bzhag go//g.yon du ni mchod yon gyi snod gnyis rtsa ku sha dang spos chur sbyar ba blugs te bzhag la
94b3(rgyud): gcig ni 'phags pa mi g.yo bas zlas so//gcig ni rdo rje dam pa sbyin gyis zlas so//zhug thabs kyi 'khor yug tu rtsa ku sha bskor ba'i thabasu bkram mo//bdag rang gar 'dug pa'i zhing yang rtswa ku sha bskor ba'i
94b4(rgyud): thabasu gding ngo//shing u dum 'ab ra dang/a shwad tha'i dgang gzar dang mar bzhus pa yang mdun du bzhag go//de nas me thab khung tsan dan gyis bskus te/metog dkar po dri zhim pa'i
94b5(rgyud): sil ma yang bkram ma zam ze'i khyim nas me blangs la/khro'i rgyal po'i gsang gtor byas te/shing blang sha dang/u dum 'bar ba'i bud shing la rlung yab kyis me spar ro//der kha byang phyogasu
94b6(rgyud): ltas te 'dug la/'phags pa gnyis byug spos kyis skus te/dam tshig gi phyag rgya la sogs pa bcings la bdag nyid rnam par snang mdzad chen po'i sku chu'i dkyil 'khor la gnas pa ci lta ba bzhin du phyag rgya
94b7(rgyud): dang gsang sngags kyis byin gyis rlabs pa'o//de nas me'i thab khung gi zhabasu chu'i dkyil 'khor bsaMs la/me'i dbus su sngar bzhin du yige bzhag pa las me'i gzugasu gyur pa/dung dang metog kun dang
94b8(rgyud): zla ba'i mdog 'dra ba thor tshugs can/mig gsum pa lag pa bzhi pa/lag na 'grang 'phreng dang /ril ba spyi blugs dang/be con thogs pa/mi 'jigs pa sbyin pa/gos dkar po bgos pa/srad bu dkar
95a1(rgyud): @#//po se ral khar thogs pa/snying kha su ma mdo'i ris mtshan can//yi ge ras mtshan pa/me lce dkar po 'phro ba/ye shes kyi rang bzhin can de lta bu'i me bsaMs la/'phags pa mi g.yo bas sngag+sa
95a2(rgyud): pa'i chu sngar bshaMs pas/me la bskor ba'i thabasu gsang gtor bya'o//rdo rje pad ma sbyin gyis kyang lan gsum du bya'o//na ma sa man ta bu ta nan/A+o~M maM swAhA//rdo rje dam pa byin gyi'o//de nas me la rang gi gsang
95a3(rgyud): sngags kyis byug spos dang/metog laswogs pa dbul lo//de nas mngar gsum la sreg lugs gsum byin te/dgang gzar chad bas bzung la mar gyi dgang gsum sbyin no//de nas shing u dum 'bar raM/
95a4(rgyud): a shwad tha'i me rtse mo'i yam bsreg shing gnyis mar la snyugs pa bdun sreg go//de nas yang mar gyis dkang lugs gsum byin la/gsang gtor yang snga ma bzhin bya'o//na ma sa man ta pu
95a5(rgyud): ta nAn;A+o~M ba ra ta ag na ye shan ti ku ru swAhA//de bzhin du lha thaMs cad la yang so sor sbyin sreg byas la gsang gtor bya'o//na ma sa man ta bu ta nan /oM ma ha shan ti ka ra sra sha ma d+har
95a6(rgyud): ma nir dza ha/a b+ha ba swAb+ha ba d+harmas ta bra ba ha a mu ka pya pras ting/shan ting shi bar ku ru swAhA//zhi ba'i gsang sngagaso//de nas sangs rgyas thaMs cad bsams la nang gi bdag nyid rnam par dag
95a7(rgyud): pas ci lta ba bzhin du byang chub seMs bsgom mo//de nas zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la rang gi rigs sngags bzhag ste/rang gi lha'i phyag rgya bcings la rang gi lha'i gzugasu bsgom mo//de nas yang snga ma
95a8(rgyud): bzhin dgang blugs gsum laswogs phul te/bskor ba' thabasu gsang gtor yang byas la/bdag gi rgyud las byung ba'i rdzas ji srid ma skyo ba'i bar du sbyin bsreg bya'o//skyod par gyur pa na rdzogs
95b1(rgyud): pa'i sangs rgyas kyi sku la bltas te yang byang chub kyi seMs bsgom mo//de nas mchod yon phul la lha rnaMs la phyag byas te slar gshegasu gsol lo//lha thaMs cad sku gshegasu gsol nas/phyag rgya'i
95b2(rgyud): phyogs rnaMs gos sar bas g.yogs la/phyi de nyid yang snga ma bzhin du lha rnaMs so so'i dkyil 'khor la gnas par bsaMs te/cho ga'i rim pa de nyid nuM zhi bar gyur gyi bar du bya'o//cho ga shes pa'i sngag+sa
95b3(rgyud): grub pa/gang zhig cho ga 'di yis su//le lo med par zhi byas nas//zhi ba myur du thob par 'gyur//zla ba phyed kyis khams ris cig//nges pa nyid du zhi bar 'gyur//rgyal po la ni zhag bcu gnyis/
95b4(rgyud): yul phyogasu ni zhag bcuso//de la zhi ba'i cho ga'i las byed pas//cho ga 'di ltar spyad par bya'o//gos dkar dgos shing rtsa ma ni//byang chub seMs dpa' rab tu gzhol//ku sha bting
95b5(rgyud): ba'i stan la nyal//de las byas pa'i sang byed thogs//nas 'tshal btsos pa 'am 'o ma 'thung//yid ni sbyin sreg byed la 'jug/dus gsum sbyin sreg zlas brjod gyis//zhi ba'i cho ga spyad
95b6(rgyud): par bya'o;;zhabs sil rdo rje mchog sbyin gyis//gsang gtor mi g.yo mgon gyiso//'di ni re shig mdor bsdus nas//sbyin sreg cho gar sgrags pa yin//rnam par snang mdzad chen po mngon par
95b7(rgyud): rdzogs par byang chub pa rnam par sprul pa byin gyis rlob pa las/zhi ba'i sbyin sreg gi cho ga rim par phye ba'o////de nas yid gcig bsdus de nas gzhan yang skal pa chen po//sngags la mkhas
95b8(rgyud): pa rnaMs kyis sngon sems can rnaMs la thugs brtse nas/sbyin sreg rgyas pa che byed pa//phan par bzhed nas bshad pa yang//yid gcig bsdus te mnyam par gyis//de bas rnam pa thaMs cad du;;rgyas pI
96a1(rgyud): //cho ga spyad par bya//grong ngam yang na ljongs dag gis//shar gyi phyogasu gnas bcas nas//snga ma bzhin du nges pa yi//sa gzhi legs par rtag par bya//ri'i rtse 'am phyugs blas
96a2(rgyud): sam;;mchod rten ring sel can dag tu//rgyas pa'i las ni bsgrub byed pa'i//dkyil 'khor shes rab can gyis bri//mkhas pa'i las kyi cho ga yis//sa ni legs par brkos nasu//dbus su
96a3(rgyud): rin chen 'bru dang ni//sman dang bcas bsres bzhag bya ste//der yang sa ni ser po yis//sa ni legs par bkang bar bya//mnyam zhing legs par bcags nas ni//de'i 'og tu dkyil 'khor bri//
96a4(rgyud): 'dir yang re shig sngags pa cho ga la mkhas pas//bdag nyid rdo rje dam pa sbyin//rang gi dkyil 'khor la gnas par//de'i gsang sngags dang phyag rgyas byin gyis brlabs la/'phags pa
96a5(rgyud): mi g.yo bas 'tshams bcad pa dang/sa yongasu bzung bar bya'o//zla ba yar gyi ngo'i tshes gsum 'am/lnga 'am bdun nam/yang na zla ba nya la yang rung ste/gza' bzang po gza' lag pa 'am phur
96a6(rgyud): pa 'am;rgyu skar gro zhun dang mon gru dang/snron dang snar ma la bdag nyid rnam par snang mdzad chen por byin gyis brlabs te/srad bu ser po blangs na grangs dang tshad snga ma bzhin du dkyil 'khor thig bdab po//
96a7(rgyud): de nas dkyil 'khor bskor te nang du zhugs la/bcom ldan 'das ma shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin ma klags la phyag kyang byas te/yang bdag nyid rnam par snang mdzad du byin gyis brlabs la/mtshon rtsi ser po
96a8(rgyud): blangs te;der ni yi ge ka bzhag pa//de tshe thub pa gser thub ni//gser gyi kha dog 'bar dang bcas//ting nge 'dzin gnas shing sdig bcom pa'o//de nas dkyil 'khor rta babs can bris la//nub phyogs kyi
96b1(rgyud): sgo rtag tu bya'o//phyi rol ri mo gru bzhi pa gsum gyis bskor la//dkyil 'khor du lha rnaMs bkod pa snga ma bzhin du bya'o//de la khyad par ni 'di yin te/'phags pa namakha'i snying po'i gnasu kun tu bzang po rin
96b2(rgyud): po che'i rgyal mtshan gyi phyag rgya yig sa dang bcas par bzhag go//sa mdzod spu'i gnasu 'phags pa rmugs 'dzin shing thog gi pi tsi bu ri phyag rgya dang /yige dza bdud rtsi'i mtshan nyid can dang bcas pa bzhagodza;
96b3(rgyud): 'phags ma khro gnyer can gyi gnasu grags ldan ma metog pri yang ku'i phyag rgya yig yang dang bcas pa bzhag go/yA//'phags ma gos dkar can gyi gnasu ni/dpal chen mo 'phags ma dkar mo zhes bya ba pad ma'i
96b4(rgyud): phyag rgya dang /yige 'brur bzhag go/shri/'phags pa gnod mdzes kyi gnasu ni/dbung bzangs rdo rje'i phyag rgya dang /yige'i 'brur bzhago/hU~M:bdud rtsi thal sbyor gnasu ni/rdo rje'i sde rdo rje'i phyag rgya
96b5(rgyud): dang yi ge'i 'brur bzhag go/sa/gnas gtsang ma pa rnaMs//kyi gnasu ni/rgya byin dang 'jig rten skyong ba bzhin dgod do//de nas phyi rol gyi ri mo gnyis nang logs kho ra khor yug dang /kwa thaMs cad
96b6(rgyud): du rdo rje bri'o//de nas dkyil 'khor gyi g.yas logasu 'khor ba'i nang du me'i thab khung bya ba ni/gru bzhi lham pa kha ran can rgya ni khru do'i tshad du bya/rdo rje yis ni 'khor bar bskor//zabasu khru gang tsam gyi tshad/
96b7(rgyud): 'jim pa ser po skyang nul sku//pad ma'i phyag rgya 'dzub gang tshad//'phang du sor bzhi'i tshad du bya//dus gsum ba glang gi lci ba sa la ma ltung ba sku'o//de nas snga ma bzhin du dkyil 'khor gyi bdag po'i
96b8(rgyud): lha laswogs pa rnaMs dang /mtshan ma ci lta ba bzhin du bsaMs te /byug spos dang metog laswogs pas mchod par bya'o//de la khyad par 'di yod de/gang gi tshe bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad gnas su
97a1(rgyud): @#//gsol ba dang/byug spos dang me tog la swogs pas mchod par byed pa de'i tshe/bdag nyid rdo rje dam par byin gyis brlabs te bya'o//'dir byug spos dang/me tog la swogs pa'i khyad par

97a3(rgyud): las gzhan pa dri zhim pa//ser po dag ni dbul bar bya//gur gum a ka ru dang sbyar//sha kha ra dang sbyar ba dag//rgyas pa'i las rnaMs 'grub bya'i phyir//gdug spos mkhas pas dbul bar bya//gu gul
97a4(rgyud): dang ni tsan dan yang//mar dang sbyar ba gdug spos mchog/sra rtsi pog ni gur gum bsreg pa//gdug spos sngags la mkhas pas dbul//mar me snod ni ser gyi zangs//yang na kham pa'i ser po

98a2(rgyud): bsaMs te;snga ma bzhin du bsreg lugs phul nas/bdag gi dngos grub bsgrub pa las/bshad pa'i rdzas las sbyin bsreg bya'o//thog mar oM zhes brjod la/'jug tu grags pa dang/dpal dang longs spyod

98a8(rgyud): yang rung;lhung gzed dang ni mkhar sil lam//yang na rdzas ni gang na gang 'dod pa//dkyil 'khor 'dir ni bsgrub par bya//rnam par snang mdzad chen po mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa rnam par

98b8(rgyud): bya ste;;dkyil 'khor kun tu bri bar ni//khru gsum yang na khru lnga 'am//yang na bdun 'am dgu yang rung//gsang sngags dngos grub rjes 'thun par//rnam pa gsum du dbye bar bya//lha rnaMs dgod pa snga ma bzhin;

99b2(rgyud): phul te/snga ma bzhin du bdag gi lha gzugs bsaMs la gzhag ste/de bzhin du sreg lugs phul la/cho ga las su byung ba'i rdzas rnaMs sbyin bsreg bya'o//'jug tu yang byang chub kyi sesaM sgom pa laswogs
99b3(rgyud): pa'i cho ga de bzhin du bya'o//cho ga 'di la yang dkyil 'khor gyi yo byad thaMs cad dmar por bya'o//me'i gnas kyang dus gsum du tsan dan dmar pos byugs shing /metog dmar po sil ma bkram//dpal ldan

105a3(rgyud): bstan par bzhed pas/dbu'i gtsug tor nas chos kyi mdzod zhes bya ba seMs can gyi khams mtha' yas pa'i seMs kyi thibs po snang bar byed pa'i 'od zer gyi dra ba byung ngo//de nas 'od zer chen po'i dra ba bkod pa des
105a4(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa' byin gyis brlabs rgya chen po rnam par rol pas/chos kyi dbyings kyi pho brang namakha'i khams ltar rgya che ba na bzhugs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnaMs la skor ba byas nas/seMs can
105a5(rgyud): thams cad kyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thaMs cad du snang bar byas te/seMs can gyi khams ma lus par yongasu skyab ba la sbyar nas/seMs can dmyal ba dang dud 'gro dang /yi dgas kyi 'gro ba

105a7(rgyud): gshegs pa bsod naMs kyi stobs byin gyis rlabs rgyu mthun pa'i bsam gyis mi khyab pas byin gyis rlabs te/chos kyi dbyings kyi dgongs pa ma rdzogs pas snying rje chen po'i snying po 'byung ba'i mdzod ces bya ba/
105a8(rgyud): seMs can gyi khams ma khams ma nges pa yongasu skyob pa'i snying po'i dkyil 'khor gyi rgyal po'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoMs par bzhugaso//snyoms par bzhugs nas kyang/phyag na rdo rje la sogs pa rdo rje 'dzin sangs rgyas kyi
105b1(rgyud): zhing bcu'i rdul phra rab mnyam pa snyed pa bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu dag seMs can gyi khams ma nges pa yongasu skyob pa snying po'i rgyal po/de bzhin gshegs pa thaMs cad 'byung ba zhes bye ba dkyil 'khor gang la

105b3(rgyud): stsal pa dang/phyag na rdo rje 'dzin sangs rgyas kyang zhing bcu'i rdul phra rab dang mnyam pa de dag gis mgrin gcig tu smras/bcom ldan 'das de'i dus la bab cing tshod la bab ste/bcom ldan 'das legs legs
105b4(rgyud): kyis;bcom ldan 'das kyis bdag cag la thugs rtse ba'i slad du/dkyil 'khor chen po bkod pa byin gyis brlab pa gang lags pa de bshad du gsol//chos rje chos kyi dbang phyug ste//chos kyi
105b5(rgyud): mnga' bdag mthar byed bral//ye shes che mnga' ston pa ste//sa'i dbang phyug gcig pu lags//dngos grub tshogs kyang grub pa ste//bur las rgyal zhing srid mthar phyin//sman pa'i rgyal
105b6(rgyud): po chen po de nyid mkhyen//'gag pa mi mnga' chad mi mnga'//mtha' gnyis rnam par spangs pa legs//de bzhin nyid dang yang dag mtha'//spros med rtog par thugasu chud//skyabs dang gnas cig dang

105b8(rgyud): rdo rje 'dzin de kun//rtag tu yid rab dga' ba yis//mtshan ni yang dag sgrags byas nas//rnam par 'brel pa gsol ba 'debs//kun mkhyen dpa' chen snyur bral zhing//de dag gis gsol thugs
106a1(rgyud): chud nas;;snying rje chen po'i snying po ni//'byung ba'i dkyil 'khor bshad byas gsungs//de nas gsang ba'i bdag po phyag na rdo rjes bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la phyag 'tshal nas/

106b8(rgyud): med 'gyur cing//seMs can khams ni ma lus pa//yongasu skyabs pa'i phyir rtson pa//mkhas pa des ni sangs rgyas kyi//dkyil 'khor dag ni 'bri bar 'os//mtshan rnaMs thams cad yongs rdzogs brtan//yid bzhungs

110b1(rgyud): 'byung ba'i phyag rgya 'am/dam tshig gi phyag rgya bcings te stong pa'i ngo bo nyid kyi rjes su song ba rnaMs/sangs rgyas dang byang chub seMs dpa'i sdom pa dang/byang chub kyi seMs 'dzin du bzhug go//de nas bla na med pa'i ye shes kyi ming

135a1(rgyud): /par bya;;de nas sbyor ba shes pa yis//de nas ri lu gcig blangs nas//gang dag mkhon par bya ba ni//de dag gcig gi spyi bo la//ri lu 'am yang na phye ma dag//mkhon par gyur pa'i phyir gdab po;;mkhon

145a3(rgyud): sgrub pa pos ni nub mo bsgrub//'bar ba de ni blangs nasu//namakha' 'phar ba gdon mi za//snga ma bzhin du grub rnaMs kyis//rtso bor gyur zhing 'dod gzugs can//du ba tsha la swogs pas kyang//de'i 'bras bu sngon bzhin thob//mtshon cha'i mchog
145a4(rgyud): rnaMs 'grub pa ni//de ltar mdo ru bshad pa ni//de nas rtog pa las byung pa'i//dngos grub gzhan yang bshad par bya//ci ltar khro bo mchog ni//nyid kyi mdo ru bshad pa'o//yidags mang po zhes bya ba'i//dkyil 'khor thog mar byas
145a5(rgyud): nas su//rma med pa'i ro blangs nas//mkhas pas khrus byas legs par brgyan//me tog pos kyis mchod nasu//sha dang bcas pa'i gtor /ma sbyin//las ni dur khrod dag tu bya//'di ni gzhan du yong mi bya//metog
145a6(rgyud): bkram pa'i gnas lam du//de ni lho phyogs mgo bstan bzhag//thod pa bzang po zas mchog gis//rab tu rkang pa byas nas ni//zas mchog 'di'i nang du yang//tshil chen dag gis blugs par bya//mkhas pas de dag de'i tshe//gzar bus
145a7(rgyud): ri mo khar blugs la//khro bo'i sngags kyis btab pa yis//blugs pa rgya ni tshang byas na//de'i lce yang 'byung bar 'gyur//de nas mtshon gyis bcad par bya//lce ni rtsa bar gcad pa las//khro bo'i mthu ni myur ba ru//de nas lce nyid ral gri
145a8(rgyud): gzugs//bzang po sum bcu rtsa gnyis 'gyur//de nas ral gri blangs nasu//grogs rnaMs la ni re re sbyin//sgrub po yis blangs nas ni//de ma thag tu 'grub par 'gyur//rigs 'dzin dag ni thaMs cad kyis//kun tu bkur ba'i rgyal por 'gyur/

145b2(rgyud): langs nas//legs par khrus byas rgyan gyis brgyan//dkyil 'khor de yi bzhag pa ste//de'i snyig khar de 'dug nas//stong rtsa brgyad du zlas par bya//khro bo'i mchog tu gyur pa'i sngags//yige bcu bdun de zlas nas//mi ro de ni
145b3(rgyud): langs par 'gyur//a rga legs par dbul bar bya//de'i mgo lag g.yon gyis ni//de ma thag tu reg byas nas//ldong ros mi yi mdog 'dra ba//de nas bdag nyid chen po yi//kha nas myur du 'byung bar 'gyur//sgrub pa pos ni de

145b5(rgyud): 'dzin rnaMs ni thaMs cad kyis//thog mar 'gro ba de ga 'gyur//rdzas rnaMs thaMs cad grub par ni//gyur nas 'di la de nas yang //dkon mchog gsum la cha cig dbul//de nas mkhas pa bdag nyid bkur//grogs po rnaMs ni thaMs cad

145b7(rgyud): de'i 'og tu rdzas de blang//grogs po rnaMs la rim pa bzhin//rdzas de byin nas 'phur bar 'gyur//byin nas cho ga 'di rnaMs spyad//de ltar ma byas grub pa yang//de yi rdzas de 'phrogs par 'gyur//de bas dngos grub mchog rnams la//
145b8(rgyud): kun tu ci skad bshad bzhin pa//de nas gzhan yang a 'dza na//bzang po sgrub pa bshad par bya//dgos pa thaMs cad 'grub pa'i phyir//dam pa 'di ni bshad par bya//zhag gsum snyung bar 'dug nas su//dur khrod dag tu dkyil 'khor gyi/
146a1(rgyud): //de nas cho ga snga ma bzhin//de'i gtor ma'i las bya'o//de nas mi ro sregs pa yi//thal bas bdag nyid bsrung bar bya//de yi bdag nyid byugs nasu//grogs po mchog ldan dpa' bo yis//a 'dza na'i bzang

146a4(rgyud): de las gso ba ni//ring por mi thogs gdon mi za//'di dag grub par ma gyur na//mig dag legs par skus nas ni//ltas pas 'gro ba dbang du 'gyur//pus mo btsugs shing mgo bo btud//a 'dza ni'i rtog pa bzhin//

146a8(rgyud): pa 'byin//rtag tu 'dod pa'i gzugs dang ldan//khro bo bzhin du de spyod 'gyur//de bzhin du ba 'byung na yang//gdon mi za bar mi snang bar//mchog tu 'gyur ba rab tu bshad//sgrub pa po'i gos de ni//de bzhin tsha bar gyur na yang//

146b3(rgyud): yongs kang nas//mar gyi ngo'i mtshan mo bsgrub//de nas changs pa mdun phyung ste//sngags kyis lan bdun zlas nasu//lha'i bzang mo 'di zhes shig//byas nas de'i rgyab tu dor//de nas changs bu gzhan yang bdun//sngags kyis

150a2(rgyud): byed 'gyur pa//chu yis nges par rtong ba dang//lcag gam yang na rkang pa yis//khro bos ci skad gsungs pa bzhin//the tsom med par sos par 'gyur//de ltar kun du yid kyis bsaM//yang na tshig gis skul bar bya//ci ltar khros pa med
150a3(rgyud): pa yang//phan tshegs las ni 'grub 'gyur te//dran pa tsam gyis 'ba' zhig gis//byed pa po'i las de grub//de nas snod la 'bab pa yang//las gzhan 'di ni rab tu bya//gzhon nu mtshan dang ldan 'gyur zhing//nyes pa'i skyon med yan lag
150a4(rgyud): tshad//shin tu bkrus te rgyan gyis brgyan//ba las byung ba'i lnga yang blud//de'i 'og tu snyung byaste//nam nangs pa dang yang bkrus la//dri zhim pa yi dris byugs la//gos rnaMs dang ni rgyan gyis brgyan//de ltar mchod pa byas
150a5(rgyud): nas ni//shar gyi phyogasu kha ltas te//cho ga bzhin du ku sha yi//stan la bzhag la de nas ni//de'i lag pa gnyis kar yang//tsan dan rtor ma'i me tog sbyin//a kar nag po'i bdug pa sbyin//des ni myur du sngags brjod de//
150a6(rgyud): sgrub pa'i cho ga 'di yis ni//snod la 'bab pa'i las bya'o//snod las babs 'gyur de nas ni//dge ba dang ni mi dge bri bar bya//de nas byung dang 'byung ba dang//byung ba gang yin bden pa smra//seMs can chen po 'di gyis la//'di ni min zhes

150a8(rgyud): nasu;;de btang nas ni sa la 'gyel//rig pa khro bo gshin rje gshed//grub pa de nas gzhud par 'gyur//de nas snod ni blangs nasu//phyis kyang rab tu khrus bya ste//zan zar bcug la zos nas kyang//mchod pa byas te slar btango//ci skad gsungs
150b1(rgyud): pa'i las rnaMs kyis//snod la bab par byas nas ni//de nas rtog pa phyi ma yi//rtog las gsungs pa'i las rnaMs bya//de las gal te sngags pa ni//rgyal po dbang du byed 'dod na//de tshe ri mo spyan snga ru//dur khrod thal bas gzugs briste//
150b2(rgyud): de'i snying khar 'dug nasu//rtse gcig yid kyis khri zlas la//che ge mo zhig dbang 'gyur zhes//de yi ming ni cho ga bzhin//khro sngags ci skad gsungs pa'i mthar//sbyar te 'jug tu swAhA brjod//khro bo dbang du bya ba'i phyir//sbyor ba rtag tu

150b4(rgyud): ma lus par//drug stong dag gis nges par 'gyur//bu med rnaMs dang bu mo la//khri khrag dag tu zlas pa bya//de'i ci 'dod 'grub par 'gyur//de nas phan tshun skye 'dza' bo gnyis//'byed par bya ba 'dod na ni//de nas

151a2(rgyud): gshegs bsam bas bya bar bstan//de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa yi//de bzhin dngos grub thogs med par//dngos grub 'dod thob 'dod na//de ltar khro seMs mi bya'o//bder gshegs bsam pa gang med pa//de'i dngos

160a1(rgyud): //sangs rgyas spyan gyi sngags dag gis//btsud nas me tog mthor du bzhug//de nas rigs 'dzin dpal dang ldan//bdud rtsi 'khyil pa zlas nas su//'phreng dang tsan dan kar la sogs/

160a3(rgyud): de nas sngags tshig cho ga bzhin//bla ma dag la phyag byas nas//gnyis pa'i yon tan dbul bar bya'o//cho ga bzhin du dbang bskur te//khro bo gshin rje gshed pa zlas//khro bo rnam par rgyal ba 'jam
160a4(rgyud): dpal rtsa ba'i rtog pa phyi ma'i yang phyi ma zhes bya ba//khro bo'i rgyal po gshin rje gshed du rtogs pa ste le'u drug pa'o//de nas gzhan yang gshin rje gshed//sngags ni 'di
160a5(rgyud): dag zlos nas ni//mi ro dag gi gos blangs nas//de la de'i gzugs bri ba//de nas mtshon rtsi sbyin med pa//bzang po snga ma'i phyi bzhin bya//bzang po khru gang tshad du bya//
160a6(rgyud): yang na khru bzhi tshad du bya//gang tshe sngags pa bdag nyid kyis//bri bar mi nus de'i tshe//gso'i snyung ba byas nas ni//khrims mnos pa dag dri ru bzhug//shi ba'i ro'i steng na bzhugs//
160a7(rgyud): phyag ni bcwo brgyad rnams kyis brgyan//zhal drug zhabs kyang drug du bstan//'jigs pa gnon pa 'jigs 'jigs blta//zhal cig bzhad cing zhal cig na//ljags 'dril zhal cig

160b3(rgyud): gsum pa yin//te zhes bya ba bzhi pa yin//ka zhes bya ba lnga pa yin//ya zhes bya ba gsang ba drug//man zhes bya ba bdun pa ste//ta ni brgyad par shis par bya//ka ni dgu ste hUM dang bcus;;de bzhin
160b4(rgyud): phaT ni lhag ma ste//mi mnyam sngags ni bcu gcig pa'o;;'jam dpal gyi ni khro bo mchog//gshin rje gshed ces bya ba 'di//rtog pa phyi ma'i phyi ma ste//sangs rgyas kun gyis mngon

160b7(rgyud): gnas dag ci ltar yin par bshad//dur khrod yang ni yin par rung//tshang tshing pad mo'i mtsho 'bram du//khros shing gshin rje gshed po ni//zlas na dngos grub thob par 'gyur//ku sha rtswa'i bdan
160b8(rgyud): la 'jug;;ri mo la ni phyogs 'dug la//lag pa gnyis ka dris byugs la//ru rag sha'i phreng ba thogs//las gnyis kyang dris byugs te//sngags pas rtag tu zlas par bya'o//de bzhin

161a2(rgyud): la ni seMs gzhag ste//skyo ba med pa'i seMs kyis ni//zlas pa'i yi ge rim bzhin bya//'bum cig zlas pa'i tha mar ni//sgrub par bya ba bshad kyis nyon//mtsho'i 'gram du dkyil 'khor ni;dur khrod
161a3(rgyud): mang po bri bar bya//de'i nang du ri mo dkram//seMs dpa' mtshan mo bzhugs nas ni//grogs bzang seMs ni ma rmongs par//gu gul bdug pa de yis sreg//zlas shing metog 'dza' ti stong//

161a5(rgyud): grub 'gyur nas//gang 'dod pa'i las rnaMs byed//gal te grub par ma gyur na//gzhan yang gcig 'bum zlas par bya//de nas de'i mtha' mar sgrub//des kyang ma grub gsum po la;

161a8(rgyud): sa yang rab tu 'gas par byed//'bab chu dag ni skyems par 'gyur//dpung rnaMs mi g.yo gnon par byed//khrag gi char yang 'bebs par byed//stong ngam yang na 'bum yang rung//de ma thag tu
161b1(rgyud): gsod par nus//gnon pa dang ni rmongs pa dang/de ma thag tu 'bebs par byed//shing rta 'am yang na klu dag gam//glang po myos la gnon ba nus//pha rol dbung dag 'khrug par byed/yul phyog+sa

161b3(rgyud): legs par gnon par 'gyur//dus min char yang 'bebs par byed//char pa dag kyang 'bebs par byed//klu'i dbang po dag kyang 'gugs//de ni nag pos zin pa yang//mthong ngam springs
161b4(rgyud): pa tsam gyis 'grub//ser ba gsol ba'i char pa dag//de ma thag tu 'bebs par nus//'bras bu metog mchog rnaMs dang//yal ga dag gis brgyan pa'i shing//bltas sam yang na rto
161b5(rgyud): 'phags pas//blo bur dag tu 'gyel bar byed//'di ni sbyar ba tsam gyis su//sa'i 'og kyang 'khrug par byed//de bzhin lha dang mi rnaMs dang//gnod pa chen po 'byung
161b6(rgyud): bar byed//khro bo'i rgyal bo 'di yis ni//nges bar dbang du mi 'gyur ba'i//dngos po 'das dang da ltar gyi//phyis 'byung ba dag gang yang med//lha de grub par ma gyur kyang//mthong ba
161b7(rgyud): tsam 'am mchog gis kyang//dug dang rims dang gdon laswogs//zhi bar byed pa mchog tu 'gyur//dbang du byed dang ldan pa dang//gsang dang skrad dang bya ba dang//mi 'phrul yan dza na sku
161b8(rgyud): rnaMs;;bdug dang bris byugs sbyor ba yis//las rnaMs 'di dag thaMs cad ni//zlas pa brgya byas 'grub par byed//sngags pa gter chen yod par ni//song ste bsrung bar byas nas su//

162a2(rgyud): gshin rje gshed kyi ming tsam gyis//lha rnaMs skrag par gyur pa ni//ring por mi thogs gdon mi za//ma rungs par ni bya ba dang//byad dang ltem dag bya ba dang//rmongs dang gnon pa'i dngos
162a3(rgyud): grub rnaMs//bltas pa rnam pa du mar yang//rtog pa gzhan gyi cho ga bzhin//ring por mi thogs 'grub par 'gyur//yul dang yul gyi phyogs dag tu//gnod pa dang ni nad kyang 'dzin//

177a1(rgyud): /dang bcas shing sbyor bcas pas//shes nas dngos grub thob par 'gyur//de nas gzhan yang ci rigs pa'i//tsham ngam bcu gnyis sbyor ba dag//mdo dang rgyas pa'i

177b3(rgyud): bya'o//rkang pa g.yon pa gdengs byas nas//g.yon phyogasu ni phyar zhing bskor//dpung pa brkyang zhing be con brdengs//sdigs pas gcig tu rmongs par byed//myur ba'i tsham ngam zhes
177b4(rgyud): bya ba//de nyid dpung stabs sdigs pa'o//de nyid la ni kha gdangs pa//kha gdangs pa zhes sgrags pa yin//sran dang bcas pa bzhi ni bskor//mthu rtsal chen po'i stobs su
